# Book of Job (Le Livre de Job)

**Author**: Anonymous  
**Series**: PO Tome 2  
**Columns**: pp. 574–689  

[p. 574] {#po.2.p574}

### THE BOOK OF JOB
ዘኢዮብ። "ወሀሎ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ብአሲ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ አውስጢድ ፡ ዘስሙ ፣ ኢዮብ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ብ አሲ ፡ ራትዕ ፡ ወንጹሕ ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ ወፈራሄ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ወይትገኀሥ ፣ እምኩ ሉ፡ ምግባር ፡ እኩይ ። " ወቦ ፡ ሰብዐቱ ፡ ደቂቅ ፣ ወሠላስ ፡ አዋልድ ። "'ወእንስሳሁ ፡ ሰብዓ ፡ ምአት ፡ አባግዒሁ ፣ ወሠላሳ ፡ ምእት ፡ አግማሊሁ ፡ ኃምስቱ ፡ ምእት ፣ ጽምደ፣ አልህምቲሁ ፡ ወኃምስቱ ፡ ምእት ፣ አእዱጊሁ ፡ አእኑስ ። ወብዙኅ ፡ አግብርቲሁ ፡ ጥቀ፣ ወዐቢይ ፡ ተግባሩ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ። ወውእቱ ፡ ብእሷ ፡ ቴር ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡በ ምሥራቀ ፡ ፀሐይ ። "ወየሐውሩ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ወይትጋብኡ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወይገብሩ ፡ ምሳሐ፣ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ። ወይነሥኡ ፡ ሠላሰ ፡ አኃቲሆሙ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወይበልዓ ፡ ወይሰትያ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ። ወተፈጺሞ ፡መዋዕለ ፡ ግዝኦሙ ፡ ይፌኑ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ወያስተሰሪ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወይገይስ ፡ በጽባሕ ፣፡ ወያበውእ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ በጐልቆሙ ፡ ወአሐደ ፡
There was in the land of Ausitis a man whose name was Job; and that man was upright, and pure, and just, and fearing God, and removed from every evil work. And he had seven sons and three daughters. And his livestock was: his sheep seven thousand, and his camels three thousand, and his pairs of oxen five hundred, and his donkeys five hundred; and his servants were many; and his labor was great upon the earth; and he was a notable man in the land of the dawn, in the rising of the sun. And his sons would go and gather together; and they would make a feast each day; and they would take their three sisters with them to eat and drink. And when the days of the feasts were finished, Job would send for them, and he would implore the

ላህመ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኀጢአተ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ። ወይብል ፣ ኢዮብ ፡ ለእመቦ ፡ ዘይጌልዩ ፡ ደቂቅ የ ፡ አኩየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ወከመዝ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕል ። "ወአምድኅረ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ መጽኡ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወቆሙ ፡ ቅድ መ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ። " ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔ ር ፡ ለሰይጣን ። እምአይቴ ፡ መጻእከ ። ወተሰጥዎ ፡ ሰይጣን ፣ ለእግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ወይ ቤ ፡ ኦድክዋ ፡ ለኩላ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወአንሶሰውኩ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወመጻአኩ ። ወይቤ ሎ ፡ እግዚአ ፣፡ ብሔር፣ለሰይጣን ። ዑቅ ፡ አልቦ ፡ዘትሔሊ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቀሩልዔየ ፡ ኢዮብ ። እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ። ብአሲ ፣ ንጹሕ ፡ ወጻድቅ ፣ ወፈራሄ ፣ እግዚ አ ፡ ብሔር ። ወይትገኅሥ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ምግባር ፡ አኩይ ። ወተሰጥወ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ቅድ መ ፡ እግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ። ወይቤ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ በከንቱ ፡ ያመልኮ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፣ ብሔ ር ። ”አኮኑ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፣፡ መላእከ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እምውስጡ ፡ ወእምአፍአሁ ፡ ቤቶ ፡ ወ ዙሎ ፡ ዘአውዱሂ ። ወባረከ ፡ ተግባረ ፣ እደዊሁ ። ወአብዛኅከ ፡ እንስሳሁ ፡ ዲበ ፣0፣ምድ ር ። ”ወባሕቱ ፡ ፈኑ ፡ እዴከ ፡ ወግስስ ፣፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘቦ ። ወዳእሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጽከ ፡ ይባር ከከ ። ”ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ለሰይጣን ። ናሁኬ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ዘቦ፡ መጠው ኩከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴክ ፡ ዳአሙ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ኢትግስስ ። ወወፅአ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እ
pardon for them; and he would rise early in the morning, and he would offer burnt offerings for them according to their number, and one ox for their sins, and Job would say: "Perhaps it has happened that my children have thought evil against God." And Job did so every day. And after the same day the angels of God came, and stood before God; and Satan also came with them. And God said to Satan: "From where have you come?" And Satan answered God, and said: "I have traversed the whole earth, and I have walked under the heaven, and I have come." And God said to Satan: "Wait then; have you not thought of my servant Job? For there is no man like him on the earth, pure, and upright, and fearing God, and removed from every evil work." And Satan answered before God, and said: "Does Job worship God in vain? Is it not because you have filled his house inside and outside, and everything that is around him, and that you have blessed the work of his hands, and that you have multiplied his flocks upon the earth? But stretch out your hand, and touch everything that he has; and we shall see if he will bless you to your face." And after that God said to Satan: "Behold then: everything that belongs to him I deliver into your hand; only you shall not touch

ግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። "ወእስከ ፡ ይአቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለኢዮብ ፣ ወአዋልዲሁኒ ፡ ይበል ዑ ፡ ወይሰትዩ ፡ በቤተ ፡ እ'ኑሆሙ ፡ ዘይልሀቅ ። " ወመጽአ ፡ ዘይዜንዎ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ለ ኢዮብ ። ፅምደ ፡ አልህምቲከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሔርሱ ። ወአእዱጊከኒ ፡ አአኑስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይት ረዓያ ፡ጥቃሆሙ ። "መጽኡ ፡ አለ ፡ ይፄውዉ ፡ ወማህረክዎሙ ። ወቀተሉ ፡ ደቀከ ፡በ መጥባጎት ። ወአነ ፡ ባሕቲትየ ፡ ድኅንኩ ፣ ወመጻአኩ ፡ እዜኑክ ። "ወእንዘ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ይ ዜንዎ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ክልእ ፡ ዘይዜንዎ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለኢዮብ ። እሳት ፡ ወድቀት ፡ እምሰማ ይ ፡ ወአውዐየት ፣ አባግዒከ ፣፡ ወበልዐቶሙ ፡ ለኖሎት ፡ ኅቡረ ። ወአነ ፡ ባሕቲትየ ፡ ድ ኅንኩ ፡ ወመጻአኩ ፡ እዜኑከ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ይዜንዎ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ዘይዜን ዎ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለኢዮብ ። መስተጽዕናነ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ ሰራዊት ፣ ዐገቱ ፡ አግማ ሊከ ፡ ወማሀረኩ ። ወቀተሉ ፡ ደቀከ ፡ በመጥባኅት ። ወአነ ፡ ባሕቲትየ ፡ ድኅንኩ ፡ ወመ ጻእኩ ፡ እዜኑከ ። ወእንዘ ፣ ዘንተ ፡ ይዜንዎ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ዘይዜንዎ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለኢዮብ ። ደቂቅከ ፡ ወአዋልዲከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይበልዑ ፡ ወይሰትዩ ፡ በቤተ ፣ እጉሆሙ ፡ ዘ ይልህቅ ። "ግብተ ፡ መጽእ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ነፋስ ፡ እምገዳም ፡ ወጐድኦ ፡ ለቤት ፡ እንተ ፡ አር
himself." And Satan went out from before God. And on that day the sons of Job and also his daughters were eating and drinking in the house of their eldest brother; and someone came to announce to Job, and said to him: "While your pairs of oxen were plowing, and while your donkeys were grazing near them, those who capture came, and they captured them, and they killed your children with the sword; and I alone escaped, and I come to announce it to you." And while he was announcing this, a second came to announce to him, and said to Job: "Fire fell from heaven, and it burned your sheep, and it devoured the shepherds together; and I alone escaped, and I come to announce it to you." And while he was announcing this, another came to announce, and said to Job: "The horsemen in three troops surrounded and captured your camels, and they killed your children with the sword; and I alone escaped, and I come to announce it to you." And while he was saying this to him, another came to announce, and said to Job: "Your sons and your daughters, while they were eating and drinking in the house of their eldest brother, a great wind came suddenly from the desert, and it shook the house at the four


[p. 578] {#po.2.p578}
ባዕቱ ፡ መፅዝኒሁ ። ወወድቀ ፡ ቤት ፡ ዲበ ፡ ደቂቅከ ፡ ወሞቱ ። ወአነ ፡ ባሕቲትየ ፡ድኅን ኩ ፡ ወመጻአኩ ፡ እዜኑከ ። ”ወአምዝ ፣ ተንሥአ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ወሰጠጠ ፡ አልባሲሁ ። ወ ላጸየ ፡ ሥዕርተ ፡ ርአሱ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወሰገደ ። " ወይቤ ፡ ፅራቅየ ፡ ወ ፃአኩ ፡ እምከርሠ ፡ እምየ ፡ ወዕራቅየ ፡ እገብእ ፡ ሂየ ። እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወሀበ ፡ ወእ ግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ነሥአ ። ወኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ፈቀደ ። ወይኩን ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ስሙ ፡፣ ለእግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። "”ወበኩሉዝ ፡ ዘረከቦ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘአበሰ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ቅትድመ፣
coins, and the house collapsed upon your children, and they died; and I alone escaped, and I have come to announce it to you." And after this, Iob arose, and tore his garments, and he cut the hair of his head, and he fell upon the earth, and he prostrated himself, and he said: "Naked I came out of my mother's womb, and naked I shall return there. God gave, and God has taken; and it has happened as God willed; and may the name of God be blessed." And in all that had happened to him, Iob did not sin before God, and he did not utter anything foolish against God.
እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ወኢወሀበ ፡ አበደ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ መጽኡ ፣ መላእክተ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር። ወ ቆሙ ፡ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ማአከሎሙ ። "ወይቤሎ ፡ እ ግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ለሰይጣን ፣ አምአይቴ ፡ መጻአከ ፡ አንተ ። ወይቤ ፣ ሰይጣን ፡ሶቤሃ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ሖርኩ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወአንሶሰውኩ ፡ ዙልሄ ፡ ወመጻ እኩ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ለሰይጣን ፡ ዑቅኬ ፡ ለቀ፡ልዔየ ፡ ኢዮብ ። እስመ፣ አልቦ ፡ ዘከማሁ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ምድር ። ብእሲ ፡ የዋህ ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ ወንጹሕ ፡ ወፈራሄ ፡ እ ግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ወይትገኀሥ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ምግባር ፡ አኩይ ። ወዓዲ ፡ የዋህ ፡ ወአንተ
And after this, from the same day, the angels of God came, and they stood before God; and Satan came into their midst. And God said to Satan: "Where do you come from?" And Satan then said before God: "I have gone under the heaven, and I have traversed everywhere, and I have come." And God said to Satan: "Have you noticed my servant Iob? For there is no man like him on the earth, innocent, and just, and pure, and fearing God, and removed from every evil work; and still he is innocent; but you have said: Destroy his


[p. 579] {#po.2.p579}
ሰ ፡ ትቤ ፡ ታሀጐሎ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ በከንቱ ። "“ ወተሰጥዎ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ለእግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወ ይቤ ፡ ቤዛ ፡ ማአስ ፡ ማእስ ። ኩሎ ፡ ዘአጥረየ ፡ ይሀብ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቤዛ ፡ ነፍሱ ። " ወበሕ ቱ ፡ ፈኑ ፡ እዴከ ፡ ወግስስ ፡ አፅጽምቲሁ ፡ ወሥጋሁ ፡ ወዳአሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጽከ ፡ ይባ ርከከ ። "ወይቤሎ ፡አእግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ናሁ ፡ መጠውኩካሁ ፡ ዳአሙ ፡ነፍሶዕ ፡ ዑቅ ። "ወወፅአ ፡ ሰይጣን ፣ እምኅበ ፣ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወአኃዞ ፣ ለኢዮብ ፣ ዝልጋ ሴ ፡ እኩይ ፡ እምእግሩ ፡ እስከ ፡ ርአሱ ። "ወነሥአ ፡ ገልዐ ፡ ወአሐዘ ፡ ይህክክ ፡ ቀስ ሎ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ አፍአ ፣ አምሀገር ፣ ውስተ ፡ መሬት ። "ወኃሊፎ ፡ ብዙኅ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ ትቤሎ ፡ ብእሲቱ ። " እስከ ፡ ማፅዜኑ ፡ ትትዔገስ ፡ ትብልኑ ። ”እፀንሕ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕ ለ ፡ ዓዲ ። ወአትዔገስ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሴፈው ፡ ሕይወትየ ። "ናሁ ፡ ማሰነ ፣ ዝክርከ ፡ አም ድር ። ደቂቅየኒ ፡ ወአዋልድየኒ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ ዚአየ ፡ ሐመ ፡ በሕማመ ፡ ማሕምም ። ወሠራ ሕኩ ፡ ወጻመውኩ ፡ ለከንቱ ። "ወአንተኒ ፡ ትነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ መግል ፡ ወዕጸያት ። ወ ዙሎ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ታንጾርር ። ” ወአነሂ ፡ አአውድ ፡ ወእታሕል ። ወአሐውር ፡ እምብሔ
goods in vain." And Satan answered God, and said: "Skin is the ransom for skin; all that he has acquired, man will give as a ransom for himself. But stretch out your hand, and touch his bones and his flesh, and we shall see if he will bless you to your face." And God said to Satan: "Behold, I deliver him to you; but spare only his soul." And Satan went out from before God; and a malignant leprosy took Iob from his feet to his head; and Iob took a potsherd, and he began to scrape his sore; and he sat outside the city in the dust. And many days having passed, his wife said to him: "How long will you persevere, saying: I will wait yet a few days, and I will persevere, for I have hope for my life? Behold, your memory has disappeared from the earth; and also my sons and my daughters; my womb suffered the pains of childbirth, and I labored, and I grew weary in vain. And you also are sitting on pus and worms, and you toss all night long. And I also wander, and I ramble; and I go from country to country and from house to house; and I wait


[p. 580] {#po.2.p580}
ር ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ወእምቤት ፡ ቤተ ። ወእፀንሕ ፡ እስከ ፡ የዐርብ ፡ ፀሐይ ። ከመ ፡ አዕርፍ ፡ አምጻማየ ፡ ወእምሕማምየ ፡ዘላዕሴየ ፡ ይእዜ ። ” ወባሕቱ ፡ ንብብ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አግዚ አ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወሙት ። ”ወነጸራ ፣፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ከመ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ እምአብዳት ፡ አንስት ፡ ተ ናገርኪ ። ሠናይተሰ ፡ ነሣእነ ፡ እምእደ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወአኪተኒ ፡ ኢንትዔገስኑ ። ወበኩሉዝ ፡ ዘረከቦ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘአበሰ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። "ወሰሚ ፆሙ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ አፅርክቲሁ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ እኩየ ፡ ዘረከቦ ። መጽአ. ፡ ኅቤሁ ፤፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አምብሔሮሙ ጦዞ ኤልፈዝ ፡ ቴምናዊ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወበልዳዶይስ ፡ አውኬናዊ ፡ መስፍን ። ወ ሳፈር ፡ አሜናዊ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወመጽኡ ፣ ኅቤሁ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ የኀውጽዎ ፡ ወይናዝዝ ዎ። ”ወርእይዎ ፣፡ እምርሑቅ ፤ ወኢያአመርዎ ፡ ወአውየዉ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ቃል ፡ ወጸር ጉ ፡ ወበከዩ ፡ ወሠጠጡ ፡ አልባሷሆሙ ። ወወደዩ ፡ ሐመደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ርአዕሙ ።፡ " ወነ በሩ ፡ ሰቡዐ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ወሰቡዐ ፡ ለያልየ ። ወአልቦ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘነበቦ ፡ አስመ ፡ ርእዩ ፣
until the sun sets to rest from my labor and my pain, which is now upon me. But only say a word against God and die!" And he looked at her, and he said to her: "You speak like one of the foolish women; we receive good things from the hand of God; shall we not accept the evils?" And in all that happened to him, Iob did not sin before God. And three of his friends having heard all the evil that had happened to Iob, all came near him from their countries: Elephaz of Teman, king; and Baldados of Aouken, governor; and Saphar of Amen, king; and they came together near him to visit him and console him. And they saw him from afar, and they did not recognize him; and they lamented by raising their voice, and they cried, and they wept; and they tore their garments, and they threw dust upon their heads. And they sat for seven days and seven nights, and none of them spoke to him, because they saw that his calamity was very great and too strong.
መቅሠፍቶ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወጽኑዐ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወአምድኅረዝ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ አፉሁ፣፣ ኢዮብ ። ወረገማ ፡ ለዕለት ፡ እንተ ፣፡ በቲ፣ ተወል
And after this Iob opened his mouth, and cursed the day in which he was born; and he said.


[p. 581] {#po.2.p581}
ደ ፡ "ወይቤ ። "ለትጥፋእ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ እንተ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ተወለድኩ ። ወሌሊትኒ ፡እ ንተ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ተባፅት ፡ ውእቱ ። ”ለትጽለም ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ፅለት ። ወኢይዓሥሣ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ በላዕሉ ። ወኢይምጳእ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ውስቴታ ። "” ወይርከብዋ ፡ ጽልመ ት ፡ ወጽላሎተ ፡ ሞት ። ወይምጓእ ፡ ቆባር ። ”ርግምተ ፡ ለትኩን ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወይ ምጽኣ ፣ ጽልመት ፡ ለይእቲ፣ ሌሊት ። ኢተሀሉ ፡ ውስተ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ ዓመት ። ወኢት ትጐለቅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ አውራኅ ። ”ለይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ጻዕር ። ኢይምጻአ ፡ ውስ ቴታ ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ ወሐሜት ። ዳአሙ ፡ ይርግማ ፡ ዘረገማ ፡፣ ለይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ዘሀለወ ፡ ይምትሮ ፡ ለዐቢይ ፡ ዐንበሪ ። "”ለይጽለሙ ፡ ከዋክብቲሃ ፣ ለይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ። "ወለይ ንበሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኢይሬኢዩ ፡ ብርሃነ ። "አስመ ፡ ኢዐጸዉ ፡ ሕምሰ ፡ ከርሣ ፡ ለእምየ ። " ኢይርእዩ ፡ ቤዛ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሠርቅ ። ”ዘኢያእተቱ ፡ ሕማመ ፣ አምአዕይንትየ ። "ለ ምንት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ከርሥ ፡ ኢሞትኩ ። ወወዒእየኒ ፡ አምከርሥ ፡ ዘኢተሐጐልኩ ፡ ሶቤ
"Perish the day in which I was born, and also the night in which it was said: It is a male child. May that day be darkness, and may God not seek it from above, and may the light not come for it; and may darkness and the shadow of death seize it, and may obscurity come to it. Cursed be that day, and may darkness come to that night, may it not be among the days of the year, and may it not be counted among the days of the months. May that night be of torment, and may joy and contentment not come into it; but may he who curses the day curse it, he who tears the great whale. May the stars of that night be darkened, and may they remain, where they do not see the light, because they did not close the exit of my mother's womb;
and may they not see the morning star, when it rises, because they did not remove the pain from my eyes. Why did I not die in the womb,


[p. 582] {#po.2.p582}
ሃ ። "ለምንት ፡ ጸንዐ ፡ ብረኪየ ፡ ወለምንት ፡ ተሐፀንኩ ፡ በጥብ ። ” ዕበ ፡ ሰከብኩ ፡ ይእ ዜ ፣ እምአርመምኩ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኖምኩ ፡ እምአዕረፍኩ ። "ምስለ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ አለ ፡ መ ከሩ ፡ በምድር ። ወኩነኑ ፣፡ በመጥባኅት ። "አው ፣ ምስለ ፡ መላእክት ፡ አለ ፣ አብዝኅ ዎ ፡ ለወርቅ ። አለ ፡ መልኡ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡ ብሩረ ። "ወሶበ ፡ ኮንኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፃእፃእ፣ ዘአድሀፀቶ ፡ ማሕፀነ ፡ እሙ ። ሂየ ፡ ኃጥአን ፡ ይውዒዩ ፡ በመቅሠፍተ ፡ መዐቱ ።፡ ወ ሂየ ፡ ያዐርፉ ፡ እለሂ ፣ ተጸዐሩ ፡ በሥጋሆሙ ። "ኅቡረ ፡ እለ ፣ አምፍጥረተ ፡ ዓለም ። ንኡሳን ፡ ወዓቢያን ፡ ሂየ ፡ ሀለዉ ። ”ወሕፃናት ፡ እለ ፡ ኢርኢዩ ፡ ብርሃነ ። “ወኢሰም ዑ ድምፀ ፡ዘያንሶሱ ። "ሂየ ፡ ገብርሂ ፣ ዘአድለወ ፡ ለአግዚኡ ። "ለምንት ፣ ወሀብኮ ሙ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ለምሩራን ፡ ወሕይወተ ፡ ለጽዑራነ ፡ ነፍስ ። ”እለ ፡ ይትሜነይዎ ፡ ለሞት 0፣
and having come out of the womb, why did I not perish immediately? Why did my knees grow firm, and why was I suckled by breasts? If I were lying down, I would be silent now; and if I were sleeping, I would then rest with the kings, who deliberated on the earth, and ruled with the sword; or with the princes, who gathered gold, and who filled their houses with silver. And then I would be like a fetus, an aborted child of the maternal womb. There the sinners burn in the scourge of his anger; there rest together those who have been tormented in their bodies since the creation of the world. Small and great are there, and also the children, who have not seen the light, and have not heard the noise of those who walk; And also the slave, who feared his master, is there.
Why have you given light to those who are in bitterness, and life to those who are tormented in the soul? Who desire death, and they do not find it,


[p. 583] {#po.2.p583}
ወኢይረክብዎ ። ወይፍሕርዎ ፡ ከመ፣መድፍን ። ”ወይትፌሥሔሑ ፡ለአመ ፡ አድምፅዎ ። ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ሞት ። ወዐጸዎ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ እምኔሁ ። ” እምድኅረ ፣፡ ማአረ ርየ ፡ ረከበተኒ ፡ መቅሠፍትየ ። አበኪ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ግሩም ፡ ዘረከበኒ ። " ወፍርሀት ፡ እን ተ ፡ ተሐዘብኩ ፡ ረከበተኒ ። ወእንተ ፡ ሐለይኩ ፡ ተዳደቀተኒ ። ”ወኢያርመምኩ ፡ ወ ኢያፅረፍኩ ፣ ወኢድኅንኩ ። ወበጽሐተኒ ፡ መቅሠፍት ።
and they search for it like a treasure; and they rejoice if they obtain it. The rest of man is death, and God has hidden it far from him. After my harvest my scourge met me, I weep because of a terrible thing that has happened to me. And the fear, which I had suspected, has met me; and what I did not think of, has come upon me.
ወተሰጥወ ፡ ኤልፈዝ ፡ ቴምናዊ ፡ ወይቤ ። ኢታብዝጎ ፡ ነቢበ ፡ አንተ ፡ በጻማ። አብዝኖ ፡ ነገር ፡ ምንት ፡ ይበጐዐከ ። ”አንተ ፡ ዘእምገሠጽኮሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ። ወእምና ዘዝከ ፡ እደወ ፡ ድኩማን ። "ወአምአንሣአኮሙ ፡ በነገርከ ለድውያን ። ወአምአጽናዕከ ፡ ብረከ ፡ ስኡኩናን ። ” ወይእዜ ፡ እስመ ፡ መጽአከ ፡ ሕማም ፡ ወገሰሰከ ። ለሊከ ፡ ተዐንብ
And I was not tranquil, and I did not rest, and I was not in security; and the scourge happened to me!" And Elephaz of Teman answered, and said: "Do not speak much, while you are in pain; many discourses, what do they profit you? You were the one who was to exhort many, and you were to console the weak men; and you were to raise up by your discourses the infirm, and you were to strengthen the knees of the feeble. But now, because pain has come upon you and touched you, is it therefore you yourself who have come to fail?


[p. 584] {#po.2.p584}
ዝ ፡ ገባእከ ። ”ቀዳሚሁ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ አብድ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፍርሀትከ ። ወተስፋከ ፣፡ ወእከየ ፡ ፍ ኖትከ ። ተዘከር ፡ ከመ ፣ ንጹሕ ፡ አልቦ ፡ አመ ፡ ተሐኾጐለ ። ወኢይጠፍኡ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ወኢይሣረዉ ። ”ወበከመሰ ፡ ርኢነ ፡ እለ ፡ የሐርስዋ ፡ ለኃጢአት ። ይዘርእዋ ፡ መቅሠ ፍተ ፡ የአሩ፣ሎሙ ። ወይትሐጐሉ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ወይማስኑ ፡ በመን ፈሰ ፡ መዐቱ ። ”ጣሕረ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ወንቃወ ፡ አንበሳዊት ። ወግርማ ፡ አክይስት ፣ ጠፍ አ ። ”ወሰኳዕትኒ ፡ የኀልቅ ፡ በኃጢኦ ፡ ዘይሴሰይ ። ወእኾለ ፡ አናብስትኒ ፡ ተኃለቁ ፡ በ በይናቲሆሙ ። "ዕበ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ጽድቀ ፡ ገበርከ ። ዝዙሉ፡ እመ ፡ ኢረከበከ ። ቀዳሚሁ ፡ ኢየአምኖ ፡ ዘይነግረኒ ። ”ግርማ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወመጽዐሞ ። ወይመጽኦ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ግብተ ፣ ፍርሀት ። ወአኃዘኒ ፡ ድንጋፄ ፡ ወረዓድ ። ወብሕቁ ፡ አንቀልቀለ ፡ አዕጽምትየ ። ”ወኃ
Before this was your fear not folly, and your hope … and the evil of your way? Remember that there is no innocent who has perished, and that the just do not succumb, nor are they uprooted. But, as we have seen, those who plow sin, and sow it, reap the scourge for themselves, and perish by the order of God, and disappear by the breath of his anger. The roaring of the lion and the cry of the lioness, and the terror of the dragons have been extinguished. The ant-lion perishes for lack of food, and the cubs of the lioness have scattered from one another. If you had done an act of justice, all this would not happen to you. Before this I did not believe what the terror of the night and the echo told me,
and suddenly fear comes to man. And I was seized with fear and dread, and my bones were shaken violently;


[p. 585] {#po.2.p585}
ልቀ ፡ ገጽየ ፡ እምነፍስየ ። ወአንሦጠጠኒ ፡ ሥዕርትየ ፡ ወሥጋየ ። ”ተንሣእኩ ፡ ወኢያ እአመርኩከ ጦ። ወነጸርኩከ ፡ ወአልብከ ፡ ራእየ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ አዕይንቲየ ። ዳእሙ ፡ ጽላሎትክ ፡ ወቃልከ ፡ ዘእሰምፅ ። ” ወባሕቱ ፡ አይኑ ፣ መዋቲ ፡ ዘይነጽሕ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ብ ሔር ። ወአይ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘይጸድቅ ፡ በተግባሩ ። "ለላአኩ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ኢይትአመኖሙ ።፡ ወለ መላእክቲሁኗ ፡ ፅጹብ ፡ ይትሐዘቦሙ ።፡ "'ወለአለሂ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ መሬት። ወንሕነሂ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ፅቡር ። ወረከቦሙ ፡ ብልዐተ ፡ ዕጹ ። "ወኢይሄልዉ ፡ እምነግህ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰርክ ። ወእስመ ፡ ስእኑ ፡ ረዲአ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ ተሐኾሉ ። ”ወነፍሖ ሙ ፡ ወየብሱ ። ወይትሐጐሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ጥበበ ።
my face departed from my spirit, and my hair and my body bristled. I arose, and I did not know you; I looked at you, and you had no form in my eyes; but it was your shadow, and it was your voice that I heard. But what is the mortal who is pure before God? and what is the man who is just in his works? He does not trust much in his ministers, and also he trusts with difficulty in his angels; and how much less in those who dwell in houses of mud. And we too, we are of the same clay, and he regarded them as food for the moth. They do not last from morning to evening; and because they could not help themselves, they perished,
ጸውዕ ፡ እስኩ ፡ እመቦ ፡ ዘያወሥአከ ። እመቦ ፡ እምነ ፡ መላእክቲሁ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ዘ ትሬኢ ። እስመ ፡ ለአብድ ፡ መዐት ፡ ይቀትሎ ። ወዕቡድሂ ፡ ይመውት ፡ በቅንአት ።
he blew upon them, and they dried up; they perished, because they had no wisdom. Call then! if there is anyone who answers you, and if there is anyone of his holy angels, that you may see! For anger kills the foolish, and the madman dies of envy.


[p. 586] {#po.2.p586}
አንሰ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ በቄለ ፡ ሥርዎሙ ፡ ለአብዳን ። ወበጊዜሃ ፣ ጠፍአት ፣፡ ንብረቶሙ ። ወወፅኡ ፡ እምሕይወቶሙ ። ወውሉዶሙ ፡ ይኩርፅዎሙ ፡ በውስተ ፡ አናቅጽ ፡ ወያመ ነዝዝዎሙ ። ወአልቦ ፡ ዘያድኅኖሙ ። ”ዘአሙንቱ ፡ ዘገቡ ፡ ጻድቃን ፣ ይበልዑ ። ወ ሎሙሰ ፡ ኢትፀብኦሙ ፡ እኪት ። ወይትመሰው ፡ ኃይሎሙ ። "እስመ ፡ አኮ ፡ እምድ ር ፡ ዘይወፅእ ፡ ጻማ ። ወአኮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አድባር ፡ ዘይበቀኦል ፡ ሕማም ። "ሰብእ ፡ ይትወለ ድ ፡ በሥራሕ ። ወእኾለ ፡ አፅዋፍ ፡ ያሌዕሉ ፡ ዖፈ ። ”ወባሕቱ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡እ ስአል ። ወአጹውዕ ፡እግዚአ ፡ ኩሉ ። "ዘገብረ ፡ ዐቢያተ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ አሰረ ። ወክብርተ ፡ ወመንክረ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ጐልቀ፦ ። ”ዘይሁብ ፡ ዝናመ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ምድር ። ወይፌኑ ፡ ማየ ፣ መትሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ”ዘያሌዕሎሙ ፡ ለትሑታን ። ወያነሥኦሙ ፡ ለግዱፋን ። "ወይመይ
For me, I have seen the root of the foolish germinate, and immediately their condition was consumed, and they went out of life. And their sons are slapped at the gates, and they are insulted, and there is (nothing) that saves them. What they have hoarded, the just eat; and evil makes war on them, and their strength dissolves. Because it is not from the earth that pain comes out, nor from the breast of the mountains that pain germinates; but man is born for labor, and the little ones of the birds raise their flight. But I will beseech God, and I will invoke the Master of all, who has done great things, which have no trace, and noble things and wonders, without number. He gives rain upon the earth, he sends water below the heaven. He raises the humble, and lifts up the abject. He overturns the designs of the deceivers, and of those whose feet do not act (in) uprightness.


[p. 587] {#po.2.p587}
ጥ፣" ምክሮሙ ፡ ለጐሐሉት ። ወኢይገብር ፡ ርቱዐ ፡ እገሪሆሙ ። "?ዘይእኅዞሙ ፡ ምክሮ ሙ ፡ ለጠቢባን ። ወያጠፍእ ፡ ሕሊናሆሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይፀፍርዋ ፡ ለምክር ። "መዐልተ ፡ ይትራከቦሙ ፡ ጽልመት ። ወይመረስሱ ፡ ቀትረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሌሊት ። "ወይትሐጐሉ ፡ በው ስተ ፡ ቀትል ። ወያመስጥ ፡ ድኩም ፡ እምእደ ፡ ኃያል ። "እስመ ፡ ፅቡስሂ ፡ ቦ፣ተስፋ። ወይትፈጸም ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ለዐማዊ ። "ወባሕቱ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ብአሲ ፡ ዘእግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ፡ ገሠ ጾ። ወኢታስተጽኅብ ፡ ተግሣጸ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ”እአስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ያቄስል ፡ ወክፅ በ ፡ ይፌውስ ። ወይቀሥፍሂ ፡ ወይሣሀል ፡ አደዊሁ ። "ስድሰ ፡ ያድኅነከ ፡ አመንሱት ። ወበሳብፅ ፡ ኢትረክበከ ፡ እኪት ። " አመሂ ፡ ረኃብ ፡ ያድኅነከ ፡ እሞት ። ወበውስተ ፡ፀ ብእሂ ፡ አምእደ ፡ ኀጺን ፡ ያነግፈከ ። ”ወይሴውረከ ፡ እመቅሠፍተ ፡ ልሳን ። ወኢትፈር ህ ፡ አምአኪት ፡ እንተ ፡ ትመጽእ ። " ወትሥሕቆሙ ፡ ለኃጥአን ፡ ወለዐማቂያን ። ወኢ
He takes the wise in their designs, and he extinguishes the thought of those who complicate their counsel. And during the day darkness will reach them, and in the middle of the day they will grope as during the night. They will perish in the combat, and the weak will escape from the hand of the powerful. For there is hope for the feeble, and the mouth of the iniquitous will be closed. But happy is the man whom God warns, and who does not judge the warning of God annoying. For he wounds, and then he heals; and he strikes, but his hands are clement. Six times, he will deliver you from tribulation, and at the seventh, evil will not reach you. If there is famine, he will deliver you from death, and, in war, he will snatch you from the hand of iron. And he will preserve you from the scourge of the tongue,
and you will not fear the evil to come. And you will laugh at the sinners and the unjust, and you will not fear the beasts of the reeds.


[p. 588] {#po.2.p588}
ትፈርህ ፡ አራዊተ ፡ ሕለት ። ” ወይሰነአውከ ፦ አራዊተ ፡ ገዳም ። ”ወ|ሶበ ፡ ታአምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ዳኅን ፡ ቤትከ ። ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይትነኬ ፡ አምንብረትከ ። "ወትጤይቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብዙ ኅ ፡ ዘርእከ ። ወደቂቅከኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሣፅረ ፡ ጺኦት ፡ ሥሙር ። ወተአቱ ፡ መቃብሪከ ፡ ከ መ ፣ እክለ ፡ መአረር ፡ ስሙር ። ወከመ ፡ ክምር ፡ ዘኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ይነሥኡ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ናሁ ፡ ዝአሰር ፡ዘአቅጻዕነ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሰማዕነ ። ወአንተ ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ አእምር ፡ ዘገበርከ ።
And the beasts of the desert will be in peace with you. And then you will know that your house is saved, and that nothing of your dwelling is damaged. And you will be sure that your posterity will be numerous, and that your sons will be like the grass of a fertile meadow. And you will enter into your tomb, like the grain of an abundant harvest, and like the heap where one draws at all times. This is the path that we have trodden.
"ወተሰጥወ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ወይቤ ። "ሶበ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔክሙ ፡ ይደልዋ ፡ ለመቅሠፍ ትየ ። ወይነሥኦ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ በመዳልው ፡ ለሕማምየ ። "እስመ ፡ ይከብድ ፡ እምኖጻ ፡ ሐ ይቅ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ሐሰተ ፡ ይመስል ፡ ነገርየ ። "ናሁ ፣ አሕጻሁ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ፡ ላ ፅለ ፡ ሥጋየ ። ወሕምዞሙ ፡ ይሠርበኒ ፡ ደምየ ። ሶበ ፡ እእኅዝ ፡ እንብብ ፣ ይደጉጻጸኒ ። "ቦቱ ፡ ለከንቱ ፡ ይነቁ ፡ ሐለስትዮ ። አኮኑ ፡ እንዘ ፣ የኅሥሥ ፡ ዘይበልዕ ። ወይነቁኑ ፡
this, what we have heard; but you, you know what you have done." And Iob answered, and said: "If one of you weighed my affliction, and also placed my pain in the balance! Certainly it is heavier than the sand of the shore; but my word resembles a lie. Behold that the arrows of God are upon my body, and their poison sucks my blood; and when I begin to speak, they pierce me. Is it in vain that the wild ass would bray?


[p. 589] {#po.2.p589}
ላሀም ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይቀምሕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጎል ። "'ወይትበላዕኑ ፣ እክል ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጹው ። ወበሁ ፡ ጥዑመ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ከንቱ ። ” ኢትክል ፡ አዕርፎ ፡ ነፍስየ ። ወአሬኢ ፡ ንሥሐተ ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብሐብሐ ፡ አንበሳ ። ”ሶበ ፡ ይመጽአኒ ፡ ጸሎትየ ። ወየሀበኒ ፡ አግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ፡ ተስፋየ ። ”ወአኀዘ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወፈጸመ ። ወቀተለኒ ፡ለዝሉፉ ፡ "ዕበ ፡ ተሐንጸ ፡ ዝጐርየ ። ወአእአትማየጥ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አረፋቲሁ ፡ ወኢይምሕክ ። አስመ ፡ ኢሐሰ ውኩ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አምላክየ ፡ ቅዱሰ ። "ምንትኑ ፡ ኃይልየ ፣ ከመ ፡ አእትዐገስ ። ወምንትኑ ፡ መዋፅልየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታስተዕዝዝ ፡ ነፍስየ ። ” ቦነ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ እብን ፡ ኃይልየ ። ወሚመ፣፡ዘ ብርትኑ ፣ ሥጋየ ። ” አኮሁ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ተወከልኩ ። ርሕቀ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ረድኤቱ ። " ወተሀየየ ኒ ፡ ሠሀሉ ። ወኢነጸረኒ ፡ አግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወኢኀወጸኒ ። ” አዝማድየኒ ፡ ኢያአመሩ
Is it not when he seeks what he eats? Does the bull bellow when he grazes in the stable? Or does one eat bread without salt? And is it pleasant, the vain discourse? My soul cannot rest, and I see the stench of my flesh like the rot of a lion. When will the object of my prayer come to me, and when will God give me the object of my hope? And God has begun, and may he finish, and may he kill me forever! And when my sepulcher has been built, and I will turn toward its walls, and I will not spare myself; because I have not falsified the holy word of my God. What is my strength, for me to be patient? And what are my days, for my soul to be strengthened? Is my strength the strength of stone? And is my flesh of brass? Have I not trusted in him? And his help is far from me. And his clemency has abandoned me; and God has not looked at me, and he has not visited me. And my relatives have not known me,


[p. 590] {#po.2.p590}
ኒ። ከመ፣ ውሒዝ።፣ዘነጽፈ። ወከመ ፡፣ መዕበል ፡ ዘኃለፈ ። "እልክቱ ፡ አለ ፡ ይፈርሁኒ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሠሐቁኒ ። ከመ ፡ በረድ ፡ ወአስሐትያ ፡ ዘጠግአ ። " ወአምዝ ፡ ተመስወ፡መ ዊቆ ። ወኢተዐውቀ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ነበረ ። "ከማሁ ፡ አነሂ ፡ ወፃአኩ፣፡ እምዙሉ ። ተሐጐልኩ ፡ ወፃአኩ ፡ እምቤትየሂ ። ”ታአምሩ ፡ ግዕዞሙ ፡ ለቴማን ። ወእከዮሙ ፡፣ ለባሳን ፣ ወተዐ ውሮቶሙ ።፡ "ወርቱዕ ፣ ይትኃፈሩ ፡ እለ ፡ ይትዌከሉ ፡ በንዋዮሙ ፣ ወበአብያቲሆሙ ። ከመ ፡ አንትሙ ፡መጻእክሙ ፡ ዝእአንበለ ፡ ምሕረት ። ወርኢክሙ ፡ ቀሩስልየ ፡ ወፈራህ ክሙ ።፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንት ፡ ቦሁ ፡ ዘሰአልኩክሙ ። ” ወሚመ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ዚአክሙ ፣፡ ተመ ነይኩ ። ከመ ፣ ታድኅኑኒ ፡ እምፀርየ ። ወታንግፉኒ ፡ እምእደ ፡ ዘይጌይለኒ ። " መሀሩኒ ፣፡ ወአነ ፡ አጸምአክሙ ። አመቦ ፡ ዘሳሐትኩ ፡ አጠይቁኒ ። "እስመ ፡ ሐሰተ ፣ ይመስለክ ሙ ፡ ነገርየ ፡ ዘእሙን ። ዘአኮ ፡ በኀቤክሙ ፡ እስእል ፡ ኃይለ ። "ኑዛዜ ፣ ነገርክሙሂ ፣
and they have been like the dried-up torrent, like the flowed-away wave. Those who feared me, they mocked me, like the hail and the compact frost, which then melt by heating, and one does not know where they were. It is thus that I have gone out of everything, and I have perished, and I have gone out of my house. Consider the genius of the Teman, and the malice of the Basan and their errors. And it is just that those who trusted in their goods and in their houses be confounded. Thus you have come without pity, and you have seen my sores, and you have feared. Why? Have I asked you for something, and have I desired your strength for yourselves, for you to save me from my enemy, and for you to snatch me from him who is stronger than I? Instruct me, and I will listen to you; if there is something where I have been mistaken, show it to me;
for it seems a lie to you, my word which is true, for it is not to you that I have asked for strength. Likewise the consolation of your discourses has not calmed me,


[p. 591] {#po.2.p591}
ኢይሜኅተኒ ። ወኢይሔውዘኒ ፡ ሠእሣአ ፡ ቃልክሙ ። ”አስመ ፡ ሠሐቅሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ እጓ ለ፣ማውታ ። ወተሳለቅሙ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዐርከክሙ ። "ወይእዜኒ ፡ ነጺርየ ፣ ገጸክሙ ፡ እን ዘ፣0፣ ኢይሔስወክሙ ።፡ "ንበሩ ፡ ወዳአሙ ፡ ኢትዐምፁ ። ወበጽድቅ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተነገሩ ። እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዐመፃ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልሳንየ ። ወጥበበ ፡ ይነብብ ፡ ጐርዔየ ።
nor has the eloquence of your words rejoiced me. Because you have laughed at the orphan, and you have mocked your friend. And now, when I looked at your face, I did not deceive you. Sit down, but do not commit iniquity, and speak henceforth with justice;
ቀዳሚሁ ፡ አኮኑ ፣ ጽላሎት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ለእጓለ ፡ አመ ፡ ሕያው ፡ዲበ፣ ምድር ። ወንብረቱሂ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዐሳብ ፡ ዘለለ ፡ ዕለቱ ። "ወአእማእኮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ገብር ፡ዘያ ደሉ ፡ ለአግዚኡ ፡ ወያደምፅ ፡ ጸሎቶ ። አው ፡ ከመ ፡ ገባኢ ፡ ዘይሴፎ ፡ ዐስቦ ። "ከ ማሁ ፡ አነሂ ፡ ተዐገስኩ ፣ አውራኀኅ ፡ ከንቱ ። ሌሊትየሂ ፣ ተውህሀበ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ጻዕር ። እመ ፡ ኖምኩ ፡ አብል ፡ ማእዜ ፡ እንጋ ፡ ይጸብሕ ፡ ወአመ ፡ ነቃህኩ ፡ ማእዜ ፡ ከክ
because there is no word of iniquity on my tongue, and my throat expresses wisdom. "First, is it not a shadow, the life of man on earth? and is his condition not like that of the mercenary, whose every day is his? And if not, like the slave, who honors his master, and who acquires what he asks, or like the mercenary who waits for his salary? And likewise, I too, I have been patient during useless months, and in every night a torment has been given to me. If I fell asleep, I said: When will the day dawn for me? And if I were awake, I said: When will the evening return? I am satiated with pain from dawn until the fall of night.


[p. 592] {#po.2.p592}
ዕበ ፡ ይመሲ ። ጸገብኩ ፡ ሕማመ ፡ እምነግህ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰርክ ። በጉበቱ ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ በ ዕጸያት ፡ ወመግል ። ኃለቁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አሐክክ ፡ ጸውበ ፡ ወዕንጳዕንጴ ፡ ወሐመደ ፡ ኮንኩ ። "ቀሊለ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ሕይወትየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነገር ። ወተሐጐልኩ ፡ በተሰፍዎ ፡ ከንቱ ። ”ተዘከ ር ፡ ከመ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሕይወትየ ። ወኢትገብእ ፡ ትርአይ ፡ ዐይንየ ፣፡ ሠናይተ ። ወኢይሬኢ ፡ ዐይነ ፡ ዘይሬእየኒ ። ወእመሂ ፡ነጸረኒ ፡ ኢይሄሉ ፡ እንከ ። ”ከመ ፡ ደመና ፡ ዘነጽሐ ፡ እምሰማይ ። እስመ ፡፣ እምከመ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብር ። "ኢየ ዐርግ ፡ እንከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ። ወኢያአምር ፡ እንከ'፡ ብሔሮ ። በእንተዝ ፡ አነሂ ፣ ኢ ይምሕክ ፡ አፉየ ። ወእነብብ ፡ ጻዕረ ፡ መንፈስየ ። ወእከሥት ፣ ምረረ ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ ዘላዕሌ የ ። "ከመ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ኮነኒ ፡ አንብዕየ ። እስመ ፡ አዘዝከ ፡ ዐቃቤ ፡ ላዕሌየ ። "እቤ ፡ ዐራ ትየኑ ፡ እንጋ ፡-ትናዝዘኒ ። ወያልህየኒኑ ፡ ምስካብየ ። ”ወትጌርመኒ ፣ በሕልም ፡ ወታደነ
My flesh is full of rot, of worms and of pus, and I have consumed myself by scratching the crust and the ulcer, and I have become dust. And my life was rapid like the word, and I perish in a vain wait. Remember that my life is a breath, and that it will not return so that my eye may see the good. And I will no longer see the eye of him who saw me, and if he looks at me, I will no longer be, like the cloud that has discharged itself from the heaven; because, when man has descended into the tomb, he does not go back up to his house, and he will no longer recognize his country. And it is for this that I will not spare my mouth, and I will speak of the torment of my spirit, and I will manifest the bitterness of my soul which is upon me. My tears were for me like the sea; because you have prescribed a guard upon me. I said: Is it therefore my bed that will relieve me? And will my couch divert me?


[p. 593] {#po.2.p593}
ግፀኒ ፡ በራኢይ ። ኮነ ፡ ስልጣን ፡ ለነፍስየ ፡ እምላዕሌየ ። ወአሙት ፡ ለአዕጽምትየ ። ”ቦ ኑ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሐዩ ፡ ከመ ፡ እትዐገስ ። ርሕቀ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ክንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፣፡ ሕይወትየ ። ምንትኑ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ታፅብዮ ። ወከመ ፡ ተኃውጾ ልቦ ። ”አው ፡ ከመ፣፡ትዋኅዮ ጦ። እስከ ፡ ይጸብሕ ። አው ፡ ትኩንኖ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ። "እስከ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ ኢትሜኅተ ኒ ። እስከ ፡ እውኅጥ ፡ ምራቂየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የኅንቀኒ ። ” ወእመሂ ፡ አነ ፡ አበስኩ ፡ ምንተ ፣ እንከ ፡ እክል ፡ ገቢረ ። ዘታአምር ፡ልበ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመ ፡ ሕያው። ወለምንት ፡ ፈጠርከኒ፣፤ በአምሳሊክከ ። አነሁ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ከበድኩከ ። ”ወለምንት ፡ ኢገበርከ ፡ በዘ ፣፡ ይትረሳዕ ፡ ኀጢአትየ ፡ ወበዘ ፡ ይነጽሕ ፡ ጌጋይየ ። ወይእዜሰ ፣ እወርድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኢይነቅህ ፡ እንከ ።
and will my couch divert me? And you frighten me by dreams, and you terrify me by visions. Take away my soul, and carry death into my bones. Will I live forever to suffer? And he has moved away from me; vain is my life. What is man, for you to magnify him, and for you to visit his heart? Or for you to test him until the point of the day, or for you to punish him in his rest? How long will you leave me without rest, for me to swallow my saliva, which suffocates me? And if I have sinned, what could I then do, you who know the heart of the living? And why have you created me in your likeness? Am I a burden to you?
And why have you not made my sin to be forgotten, and my offense to be erased? And now I descend into the earth, where I will no longer wake up.


[p. 594] {#po.2.p594}
"ወተሰጥወ ፡ በልዳዶስ ፡ አውኬናዊ ፡ ወይቤ ። ” እስከ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ ትነብብ ፡ ከመዝ። መንፈሰ ፡ ነቢብ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፉከ ። ”ቦኑ ፡ አግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ይኬንን ፡ ዐመፃ ። ወፈጣ ሬ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ የሀውኮ ፡ ለጻድቅ ። ”እመኬ ፡ ደቂቅከ ፡ አበሱ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ። ወፈነወ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ኃጢአቶሙ ። ”ወአንተሰ ፡ ጊሥ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወጸሊ ። "አመ ፡ጻ ድቅ ፡ ወንጹሕ ፡ አንተ ፡ ይሰምፀከ ፡ ስአለተከ ። ወየዐሲየከ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ጽድቀክ ። "ወአ መ ፡ውሔሑድ ፡ ቀዳሚከ ። አልቦ ፡ ጐልቄ ፡ ደኃሪትከ ። "ተሰአሎሙ ፡ ለቀደምተ ፡ ዓለ ም። ወሕትቶሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ በበ ፣ ሕዘቢሆሙ ። "ሣሬት ፡ ንሕነ ፣ ወኢናአምር ። ወጽ ላሎት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕይወትነ ። ” ወእሙንቱ ፡ ይሜሀሩከ ፡ ወይነግሩክ ። ወያወፅኡ ፡ ለከ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እምልቦሙ ። " ይበጐልኑ ፡ ሣዕር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ማይ ። ወይልሀቅኑ ፡ ሐመልማል ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይሰቅይ ። "ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፣ ውስተ ፡ሥርው ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣፡ ይግምድዎ ። ኩሉ ፣
And Baldados of Aouken answered, and said: How long will you speak thus? The spirit of the word is in your mouth. Does God judge according to iniquity, and does the Creator of all trouble the just? If therefore your sons have sinned before him, then he has delivered them because of their sin. But you, go early toward God, and pray: if you are just and pure, he will hear your prayer, and he will soon grant you the reward of your justice. And if your first state was meager, the last will be without number. Interrogate the first [men] of the world, and question the fathers of each of their peoples. We are a spider's web, and we are ignorant; and our life is like the shadow. And they will instruct you, and they will speak to you: and they will make you bring out the discourses of their heart.
Does the grass grow without water? And does the turf grow without being watered? And while it is in its root, without being cut,


[p. 595] {#po.2.p595}
ሣፅር ፡ ዘኢይሰቲ ፡ ማየ ፡ ይየብስ ። ”ከማሁ ፡ ደኃሪቶሙ ፡ ለዙሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ይረስፅዎ ፡ ለአግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ። ወትጠፍእ ፡ ተስፋሆሙ ፡ ለረሲዓን ። ”መዝብር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቤቱ ። ወሣሬተ ፡ ይከውኖ ፡ ማኅደሮ ። ”ወአመሂ ፡ አጽንዖ ፣ ለቤቱ ፡ ኢይቀውም ። ወዘወጠነ ሂ ፡ ኢይፌጽም ። ”ወሥሕቡብ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በታሕተ ፡ ፀሐይ ። ወበእከዩ ፡ ይኅልቅ ፡ ኅይ ሉ ። መእከለ ፡ ጾልዓት ፡ይበይት ። ወውስተ ፡ ሕዝሕዝ ፡የሐዩ ። ”ወእመሰ ፡ ውኅጦ ፣ ምድሩ ፡ ሐሰዎ ። ኢርኢኩ ፡ ዘከመዝ ። ”እስመ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ይትገፈትኡ ፡ ረሲዓን ። ወክ ልእኒ ፡ ይበቀል ፡ እምድር ። ”ወእግዚአ ፡ ብሔርሰ ፣ ኢይገድፎ ፡ ለየዋህ ። ወኢይት ሜጠው ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መባአ ፡ ኃጥአን ። ”አፈ ፡ ራትዓን ፡ ይመልእ ፡ ሠሐቀ ። ወየአምን ፡ ከናፍሪሆሙ ። ”ወጸላእቶሙኒ ፡ ይለብሱ ፡ ኅፍረተ ። ወይጠፍእ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ኃጥአን ።
Every herb that does not drink water withers. Such is the end of all those who forget God; and the hope of the impious shall perish. His dwelling shall be ruins; and his habitation like a spider's web. And if he has consolidated his house, it shall not remain standing; and what he has begun, he shall not finish. And he shall be moldy under the sun; and his strength shall be consumed in his wickedness. And he shall pass the night in the midst of the rocks; and he shall live in the gravel. And if the earth has devoured him, it denies him; I have seen nothing like it, [it says]. For it is thus that the impious shall be overthrown; and others shall sprout from the earth. But God will not reject the innocent; and he will push away all the gifts of sinners. The mouth of the upright shall be full of smiles; and his lips shall celebrate [him]. And likewise their enemies shall be clothed with shame; and the house of sinners shall disappear.


[p. 596] {#po.2.p596}
”ወተሰጥወ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ወይቤ ። አማን ፡ አአምር ፡ ከመ ፣ ከመዝ ፣ ውአቱ ። ወእ ፎ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ በኅበ ፡ እግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ። ” ወእመሂ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ይጽዕ ሮ ፡ ኢይሰጠዎ ። ከመ ፡ ኢይትዋሣአ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ቃለ ። ”እምእልፍ ፡ ጠቢበ ፡ ምክር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወዐቢይ ፡ ወኃያል ። መኑ ፡ እኩየ ፡ ከዊኖ ፡ ዘቆመ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ። ”ዘያንቀለቅሎ ሙ ፡ ለአድባር ፡ ወኢይሬኢይዎ ። ወይገፈትኦሙ ፡ በመዐቱ ። ”ዘያድለቀልቃ ፡ እመሰ ረታቲሃ ፡ ለሰማይ ። ወየሀውኮሙ ፡ ለአፅማዲሃ ። ወየዐትቦሙ ፡ ለከዋክብት ። ዘይብ ላ ፡ ለፀሐይ ፡ ወኢትሠርቅ ። ”ዘሰፍሖ ፡ ለሰማይ ፣ ባሕቲቱ ። ወየሐውር ላዕለ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ከመ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ። "ዘይገብር ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ወያስተጋብእ ። ወያርኢ ፡ ወያኃልቆሙ ፡ ለ አዜብ ። "ዘይገብር ፡ ዐቢያተ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ጐልቀ ። -እመሂ ፡ ተዐደወኒ ፡ ኢይሬኢዮ ።
And Job answered, and said: In truth, I know that it is so; and how shall the just man be pure before God? And if he wishes to torment him, he will not answer him, so as not to dispute a single word. More than ten thousand he is wise in his counsels, and great, and powerful; who is he who, being evil, has remained standing before him? It is he who shakes the mountains, and one does not see him; and he overturns them in his anger. And he makes the sky tremble, from its foundations; and he shakes its pillars. And he puts a seal on the stars; it is he who speaks to the sun, and it does not rise. It is he who has stretched out the sky, he alone; and he walks on the sea as on the earth. It is he who accomplishes many works, and gathers,
and [who] sees and consumes these from the South. It is he who does great things, innumerable. When he passes before me, I do not see him;


[p. 597] {#po.2.p597}
ወእመሂ ፡ ኅለፈኒ ፡ ኢያአምሮ ። "እመ ፡ አአተተ ፡ መኑ ፡ ያገብእ ። ወመኑ ፡ ይብሎ፣ ምንተ ፡ገበርከ ። “ውእቱ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ እመቅሠፍትየ ። አምኔሁ፣ ተዐጽዉ ፡ ዐናብርት፣ ዘመትሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ”ወአመሰ ፡ ይሰምዓኒ ፡ ቃልየ ፡ ወኢይፌክሮ ። "ወአመሂ፣ በጽድ ቅ፣ወኢሰምዐኒ ። ወእስአል ፡ ፍትሐ ፡ ዚአሁ ። "ወእመሂ ፡ ጸዋፅኩ ፡ ወአውሥአኒ ። ኢየአምን ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰምዐኒ ። ”ዓውሎኑ ፡ ይቀጠቅጠኒ ። ወብዙኀ ፡ አቀነሰለኒ ፡ ለከንቱ ። ወኢየኅድገኒ ፡ አፅርፍ ። ወአጽገበኒ ፡ ሕምዘ ። "አስመ ፡ ጽኑዐ ፡፣ ይእኅዝ ። መኑ ፣ ይትቃወማ ፡ ለኩነኔሁ ። ”ወአመሂ ፡ አፉየ ፡ ዐመፀ ፡ እምጽድቅ ። ወእመኒ ፡ ነጻሕኩ ፡ ዕጹብ ፡ ይረክበኒ ። "ወአመሂ ፡ አበስኩ ፡ እንዳዒ ፡ ለነፍስየ ። ዳእሙ ፣ ጠፍአት ፡ ሕይ
and if he passes before me, I do not know him. And if he goes away, who will bring him back, and who will say to him: What have you done? And he has not turned away from my affliction; by him were enclosed the whales that are under the sky. How much less will he hear my voice, and will he not explain it? And if I have invoked him with justice, and if he has not listened to me, I will implore his right. And if I have invoked him, and if he has answered me, I will not believe that he has listened to me. Is it the whirlwind that crushes me? He has given me numerous wounds, in vain. And he does not let me rest; and he has satiated me with poison. For he seizes with force; and who will resist his judgment?
And if my mouth has committed iniquity more than justice, and if I am pure, severe he will meet me. And if I have sinned, my soul does not know it; but my life has been extinguished.


[p. 598] {#po.2.p598}
ወትየ ። ”ወበሕቱ ፡ እቤ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወኃያል ፡ ይፌኑ ፡ መቅሠፍትየ ። "እስመ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ሞቶሙ ፡ ለኃጥአን ። ወይሥሕቅዎሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ። ወይገብእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኃጥአን ። ወ ይከድንዋ ፡ ገጸ ፡ መድልዋን ። ወአነ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ አነ ። ”ወሕይወትየኒ ፡ ትቀልል ፡ እም ረዊጽ ። አለ ፡ አምሰጡ ፡ ወኢረከቦሙ ፡ እንከ ። ”ወአመቦ ፡ ሐመር ፡ አሰረ ፡ ፍኖቱ ። አው ፡ ንስር ፡ ዘይሠርር ፡ ወየኅሥሥ ፡፣ ዘይበልዕ ። ”ወእመሂ ፡ ነበብኩ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘእበ ቀፉዕ ። ወድቀ ፡ ገጽየ ፡ በገዓር ። " ያንቀለቅል ፣፡ ኩሉ ፡ መለያልይየ ። አእመርኩ ፡ ከ መሰ ፣ ኢተኅድገኒ ፡ ከመዝ ። እመኬ ፡ ኃጥእ ፡ አነ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ኢሞትኩ ። "እመኒ ፡ ተኀፀብኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ በረድ ፡ ወነጻሕኩ ። ወአንጻሕኩ ፡ እደዊየ ። ”ብሕቁ ፡ ትጠምፀኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ርስሐት ። አስቆረረኒ ፡ ልብስየ ። "”ሶቤሁ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘይትዋቀሠኒ ። ወ
But I say: The great and the powerful sends my affliction. Because the death of sinners is evil; and they will laugh at the just. And he will hand them over to the sinners; and the iniquitous judges will veil their faces. And I, what am I? And my life is faster than the race of those who flee, and meet no more; or like the trace of the path of the ship, or of the eagle, which flies and seeks what it eats. And if I have spoken, I derive no profit from it; and my face falls with groans. And all my limbs are agitated; but I know that he will not leave me thus. And if I am a sinner, why am I not dead? When I would wash myself, and I would be pure as snow, and I would purify my hands, you would plunge me into deep mire, and my clothes would have me in horror. If it were a man, who disputed with me, we would go together to the tribunal.


[p. 599] {#po.2.p599}
ንሖር ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓውድ ። ”ሶቤሁ ፡ ቦ ፡ ኅሩየ ፡ ዘይፈትሕ ፡ ለነ ። ወያጸምእ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ክልኤነ ። ትአእትት ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ በትር ። ወኢይፅብአኒ ፡ ግርማሁ ። ”ወ እነግር ፣ እንከ ፡ ወኢይፈርህ ። ወአልቦ ፡ ዘአአምር ።
And if there had been an equal to judge us, and to listen to our discussions; this rod would have moved away from me, and its terrors would not fight me. And I will speak then, and will not fear;
" በዘ ፡ ትጹዐር ፡ ነፍስየ ። እምዕጹብ ፡ አወሥእ ፡ ቃልየ ። እነብብ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትጠወ ቅ ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ መሪረ ። "” ወአቤሎ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ኃጢአት ፣ ኢትመህረኒ ። ወለም ንትኬ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ኩነንከኒ ። ”ይሜንየከኑ ፡ አመ ፣ አበስኩ ። እስመ ፡ ተሀየይከ ፡ ተግባ ረ ፡እደዊከ ። ወነጸርከ ፡ ምክረ ፡ ረሲዓን ። ” ወከመ ፡ መዋቲ ፡ ትኔጽር ፡ ወትሬኢ ። "ወ መዋዕሊክኒ ፡ ከመዘ ፡ ሰብአእኑ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወዐመቲከኒ ፡ ከመዘ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመ ፡ ሕያውኑ። "ከመ ፡ ትኅሥሣ ፡ ለኃጢአትየ ። ወትሕትት ፡ ጌጋይየ ። ”ታአምር ፡ ከመሰ ፣ ኢአበስ
and there is nothing that I know. Because my soul is tormented, I will barely answer with my voice, and I will speak although my soul is pressed bitterly. And I will say to God: Does my sin not instruct me? And why have you thus condemned me? Do you find it good that I have sinned? Why have you despised the work of your hands, and have looked with pleasure upon the counsel of the impious? And like the mortal, do you look and see? Are your days like those of man, and are your years like those of the living, so that you search for my sin,
and that you inquire about my fault? But you know that I have not sinned.


[p. 600] {#po.2.p600}
ኩ ። ወበሕቱ ፡ መኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘያመስጥ ፡ እምእዴክ ። እደዊከ ፡ ፈጠራኒ ፡ ወገብራ ኒ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አፍለስከኒ ፡ ወነፃኅከኒ ። ” ተዘከር ፡ ከመ ፡ እምፅቡር ፡ ፈጠርከኒ ። ወታገ ብአኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መሬት ። ”ወከመ ፡ ሐሊብ ፥፡ ሐለብከኒ ። ወአርጋእከኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ግብነት ። ”ወአልበስከኒ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወማእሰ ። ወአሰርከኒ ፡ አዕጽምትየ ። "ወወሀብከኒ ፣ ሣሀለ ፡ ወምሕረተ ። ወኃውጾትክ ፡ ዐቀበኒ ፡ መንፈስየ ። ”ወዘንተ ፡ዘኀቤከ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ አአ ምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትክል ። ወአልቦ ፡ ምንትኒ ፡ ዘይሰአነከ ። ”ሶቤሁ ፡ አበስኩ ፡ አም ዐቀብከኒ ። ወኢያንጻሕከኒ ፡ እምኃጢአትየ ። ወአመሂ ፡ አበስኩ ፡ አሌ ፡ሊተ ። ወእመ ሂ ፡ ጸደቁ ፡ ስእንኩ ፡ አንቃዕድዎ ። ጸገብክዋ ፡ ለኃሳር ። ተሠገርኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ዘይቀትሉ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ተአተኒ ፡ ወብሕቁ ፡ ትጠስየኒ ። ወእምጥንቱ ፡ ክዕበ ፡ ተሐተኒ ።
But who is it who will escape your hand? Your hands created me, and fashioned me; and after that you transformed me, and you crushed me. Remember that you created me from clay, and that you will make me return to dust; and that you milked me like milk, and that you curdled me like cheese. You clothed me with flesh and skin; and you bound my bones. You gave me clemency and mercy; and your providence has guarded my soul. And that is yours, and I know that you can do everything; and there is nothing that is impossible for you. If I had sinned, you would observe me; and you have not purified me from my sin. And if I have sinned, woe to me; and if I have been just, I cannot look up;
I am satiated with ignominy. I was caught in a trap, like a lion that one kills; and you will stir me, and you will destroy me. And from the beginning you will resume your inquiry,


[p. 601] {#po.2.p601}
ወአምጻእከ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ። ወፈነውከ ፣ ሊተ ፡ መንሱተ ። "ለምንት ፡ እንከ ፡ ኢሞትኩ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ታወፅአኒ ፡ እምክርሥ ። ወኢትርአየኒ ፡ ዐይን ። ”“ወእኩን ፡ ከ መዘ ፡ ኢተፈጠርኩ ። ለምንት ፡ ተፈጢርየ ፡ ውስተ ፣፡ መቃብር ፡ ኢወረድኩ ። ወወሂእ የ ፡ አምከርሥ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ ኢያዕረፍኩ ። ” አኮኑ ፡ ውሑድ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይ ወትየ ። ኅድገኒ ፡ አፅርፍ ፡ ሕቀ ። ”ዘአንበለ ፡ እሖር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኢይገብእ ። ብሔረ ፡ ጽል መት ፡ ወቆባር ። ”ብሔረ ፤ ጽልመት ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ኅበ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ብርሃነ ። ወኢይሬኢ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ መዋቲ።
and you have brought against me a great affliction, and you have sent me calamity. Why then am I not dead, when you made me come out of the (maternal) womb, and the eyes would not have seen me; and I would be as if I had not been created? And why, after having been created, did I not descend into the tomb, and, after coming out of the (maternal) womb, did I not rest in the tomb? Are the days of my life not few? Let me rest a little, before I go, there from where I will not return, to the land of darkness, and of fog,
ወተሰጥወ ፡ ሳፈር ፡ አሜናዊ ፡ ወይቤ ። "በከመ ፡ ብዝኀ ፡ ትነብብ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ትሰም ዕፅ ። በብዝኀ ፡ነቢብከኑ ፡ ዘትጸድቅ ፡ ይመስለከ ። "ቡሩክ ፡ ዘይትወለድ ፡ እምአንስት ፡ዘ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋፅሊሁ። ኢታብዝኅ ፡ነቢበ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይትዋቀሠከ። "ወኢትበል፣
to the land of eternal darkness, where there is no light, and where the mortal does not see life. And Zophar of Amen answered, and said: Just as you have spoken much, so must you listen. Do you think to justify yourself by your numerous words? Blessed is he who is born of woman and whose days are few. Do not speak anymore, for there is no one who accuses you. And do not say: I am pure in my works, and I am just before him.


[p. 602] {#po.2.p602}
ንጹሕ ፡ አነ በምግባርየ ። ወጻድቅ ፡ አነ ፡ በቅድሜሁ ። ”ወእፎኑመ ፣ ይትናገረከ ፡ እግዚ አ ፡ ብሔር። ወይከሥት ፡ከናፍሪሁ ፡ ምስሌከ ። ”ወይነግረከ ፡ ኃይላ ፡ ለጥበብ ። አስመ ፣ ፅፁፍ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘላዕሌክ ። ሶቤሁ ፡ ታአምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይደልወከ ፡ ዘረከበከ ፡ አምኅበ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ በኃጢአትከ ። ”ወአሰሮሁ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፣ ትረክብ ። ወሀለው ከኑ ፡ ቀዲሙ ፡ አመ ፡፣ ይፈጥር ፡ ዘዙሎ ፡ ይመልክ ። ”ልዑል ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምንተ ፡ ትሬ ሲ። ወምንተ ፡ ታአምር ፡ ፅመቀ ፡ ቀላይ ። ”ወአምጣነ ፡ ኑኀ ፣፡ ምድር። አው ፡ ርሕባ ፡ ለባሕር ። "ወአመ ፡ ገፍትኦ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ መኑ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ገበርከ ፡ ዘይብሎ ። "ለሊሁ ፡ ያ አምር ፡ ግብረ ፡ ኃጥአን ። ወእመ ፡ ርኢየ ፡ ዐመፃ ፡ ኢያረምም ። "ወሰብአሰ ፡ ኃሣሜ ፡ ነቢብ ። ወመዋቲኒ ፡ዘይትወለድ ፡ እምአንስት ፡ ከመ ፡ አድግ ፡ ይመስለኒ ። "ወአንተሰ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ትብል ። ወታዐቢ ፡ አደዊከ ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ”ወአመሰቦ ፡ ዐመፃ ፡ ውስተ ፡
And how will God speak to you, and open his lips with you? And he will tell you the power of wisdom; because it is double that which is yours. Then you will know how that was due to you, what has happened to you from God for your sins. And will you find the traces of God, and were you before the Almighty created [the world]? The sky is high, and what were you doing, and what did you know of the depth of the abyss? And what was the length of the earth, or the width of the sea? And when he has overturned everything, who said to him: What have you done? He himself knows the work of sinners; and if he sees iniquity, he is not silent. But man is eager to speak; and the mortal, who is born of woman, seems to me to be like a donkey. And you, you say: My heart is pure;
and you lift your hands toward him. But if there is iniquity in your hands, do not approach;


[p. 603] {#po.2.p603}
አደዊከ ፡ ኢትቅርብ ። ወኢታንብር ፡ ጽልሑተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልብከ ። "ከመዝ ፡ ያበርህ ፡ ገጸከ ፡ ከመ ፡ማይ ፡ ንጹሕ ። ወአእትት ፡ ርስሐተከ ፡ ወኢትፍራህ ፡ እንከ ። ”ወትረስዓ ፡ ለሕማምከ ። ከመ ፡ ሞገት ፡ እንተ ፡ ኀለፈት ፡ ወኢትደምፅ ፡ እንከ ። ”ወጸሎትክሂ ፡ ከ መ ፡ኮከበ ፡ ጽባሕ ። ወትሠርቅ ፡ ሕይወትከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሞዐልት ። "ወትትአመን ፡ከመ፣ት ረክብ ፡ ተስፋከ ። ወትነብር ፡ ዳኅነ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ሐዘን ፡ ወትካዝ ። ”ወታዐርፍ ፡ እንከ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይፀብአከ ። ወይመጽኡ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ወይገንዩ ፡ ለከ ። ”ወትኀልቅ ፡ መድኀኒ ቶሙ ። ወየሐኾሉ ፡ ተስፋሆሙ ። ወይትመሰዉ ፡ አፅይንተ ፡ ረሲዓን ።
and do not place deceit in your heart. Thus he will make your face shine, like pure water; and he will remove your stain, and you will no longer fear. And you will forget your pain, like the wave that has passed, and that makes no more noise. And also your prayer will be like the morning star; and your life will rise like the day. And you will believe you will meet the object of your hope; and you will remain in safety without sorrow and without pain. And you will rest at last, and there will be no one to make war on you; and many will come to pay you their homage. And the salvation of sinners will be annihilated,
ወተሰጥወ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ወይቤ ። "አንትሙኒ ፡ ሰብእኑ ፡ አንትሙ ። ወበኅቤክሙኑ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ተሰልጠት ፡፣ ጥበብ ። "አነሂ ፡ ብየ ፡ ልበ ፣ ከማክሙ ። "ብአሲ ፣ ጻድቅ ፣ ወንጹ
and they will lose their hope; and the eyes of the impious will dissolve. And Job answered, and said: You too are men; and is it in you alone that wisdom is finished? And I too, I have intelligence like you. The just and pure man has been [an] object of derision.


[p. 604] {#po.2.p604}
ሕ ፡ ስላቀ ፡ ኮነ ። "ለዕድሜ ፣ መዋፅሊሁ ፡ አፅንሕዎ ፡ ያኅስርዎ ፡ በዕዳን ። ከመ ፣ ያው ድቁ ፡ ቤቶ ፡ ኃጥአን ። ”ወባሕቱ ፡ እኩይኒ ፡ ኢይብል ፡ ራነ ፡ አለ ፡ ያምዕዎ ፡ ለእግዚ ኦ ፡ብሔር ። አኮኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘያሐቶሙ ። ”ተሰአሎሙ ፡ ለእንስሳሂ ፡ ለአመ ፡ ይነግሩ ከ። ወለአፅዋፈ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ለአመ ፡ ያየድዑከ ። ”ተናገራ ፡ ለምድርሂ ፡ ለአመ ፡ ትፌክር፣ ለከ ። ወይንግሩከ ፡ ዓሣተ ፡ ባሕር ። ”መኑ ፡ ዘያአምር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ። ከመ ፡ አደ ፡ እ ግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ገብረ ። ”እስመ ፣፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ሕያው ። ወመንፈሰ፣ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ። "እዝንሰ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ይፈልጥ ። ወኾርዔ ፡ እክለ ፡ ይጥዕም ። "በብዝኀ ፡ ዓ መት ፡ ጥበብ ። ወበብዝኀ ፡ ንብረት ፡ ትምህርት ። ”ወአምኅቤሁ ፡ ጥበብ ፡፣ ወኃይል ። ሎቱ ፡ ምክር ፡ ወአአምሮ ። "እመ ፡ ነሠተ ፡ መኑ ፡ የሐንጽ ። ወአመ ፡ ዐጸወ ፡ላዕለ፣ሰ
Strangers will wait for the end of his days, so that sinners may tear down his house. But the wicked will not say: Virtuous are those who irritate God. Will he not inquire about them? Ask also the beasts, if they speak to you, and the birds of the sky, if they instruct you. Speak also to the earth, if it explains to you, and the fish of the sea will speak to you. Who is it who understands all this, that the hand of God has made? Because the soul of every living thing is in his hand, and the spirit of every man. The ear discerns the word, and the throat tastes the food. In numerous years is wisdom, and in the length of existence is science. It is from him that wisdom and power come, and it is to him (that belongs) counsel and knowledge. If he has destroyed, who will build? And if he has closed upon man, who will open?


[p. 605] {#po.2.p605}
ብእ ፡ መኑ ፡ ያር ። ”አመ ፡ ከልአ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ያየብሳ ፡ ለምድር ።፡ አመ ፡ ኃደጋ ፡ ትማስ ን ፡ ትትገፈታአ ። ”አምኅቤሁ ፡ ኀይል ፡ ወጽንፅ። ዘዚአሁ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ወምክር ። "ይወስ ዶሙ ፡ ለመካርያን ፡ ይፄወዉ ። ወሰዶሙ ፡፣ ለመኳንንተ ፡ ምድር ። "ወያነብሮሙ ፣፡ለ ነገሥት ፡ ዲበ ፡ መናብርት ። ወአቅነቶሙ ፡፣ ቅናተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐወሆሙ ፡ ”ወይፌንዎ ሙ ፣ ለማርያን ፡ ይፄወዉ ። ወገፍትኦሙ ፡ ለኃያላነ ፡ ምድር ። ”“ወይመይጥ ፡ ከናፍሪሆ ሙ ፡ ለመሀይምናን ። ወያአምር ፡ ምክሮሙ ፡ ለሊቃናት ። "ወያመጽእ፣፡ሎሙ ፡ ኀሳረ፣ ለመላእክት ። ወይሣሀሎሙ ፡ ለትሑታን ። ”ወይከሥት ፡ ዕሙቃተ ፡ እምጽልመት ። ወ አውፅኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ለጽላሎተ ፡ ሞት ። ”ያዐወክኮሙ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ፡ ወይትሐ ጐሉ ። ወይሠርዖሙ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ፡ ወይመርሖሙ ። "ወይመይጥ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ለመላእክተ፣
If he stops the sky, he dries up the earth; and if he has released it, the earth will be ravaged and overturned. Because it is from him that strength and firmness come, and to him are wisdom and counsel. And he takes away the counselors so that they may be captives and he leads the princes of the earth. And he makes the kings sit on the thrones; and he girds the belt on their loins. And he sends the diviners into captivity; and he overturns the powerful of the earth. And he changes the lips of the faithful; and he knows the counsel of the elders. And he brings ignominy to the princes; and he is clement to the humble. And he manifests the depths (by pushing aside) the darkness; and he draws them from the shadow of death to the light. And he shakes the nations, and they perish;
and he organizes the peoples, and leads them. And he changes the heart of the princes of the earth; and he has made them wander by paths that they did not know.


[p. 606] {#po.2.p606}
ምድር ። ወአዔሎሙ ፡ ፍኖተ ኢያአምሩ ። ”ወይመረስሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽልመት ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ብርሃን ። ወይተነትኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ስኩር ።
And they grope in the darkness, and not in the light; and they stagger like a drunken man.
ናሁዝ ፡ ዘርእየት ፡ ዐይንየ ። ወዘሰምዐት ፡ አዝንየ ። ”ወአአምር ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ታአም ሩ ፡ አንትሙሂ ። ወአምኔክሙሰ ፡ ኢየአብድ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፡ ብሔር፣ እነግር ። ወእትዋቀስ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ እመ ፡ ፈቀደ ። ”አንትሙሰ ፡ አቀብተ ፡ ሥራይ ፡ ዘዓ መፃ ። ወመናዝዛን ፡ ለእኪት ፡ ኩልክሙ ። ”ኢይረትዓኒ ፡ አጽምዕክሙ ። እስመ ፡ ጠፍአ ት ፡ ጥበብ ፡ እምኔክሙ ። ”ስምዑኒ ፡ቅስተ ፡ አፉየ ። ወአጽምዑኒ ፡ ፍትሐ ፡ ከናፍርየ ። ይእዜኒ ፣ አኮኑ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ትነብቡ። ወታየድዑ ፡ ጐሕሉተ ፡ ቅድሜ ሁ ። ”ወትትገኀሠ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ እስኩ ፡ ለሊክሙ-፣ ፍትሑ ። ”ሠናይሰ ፡ ዕበ ፡ የሐቱክ ሙ ። እንዘ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትገብሩ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ተኅሠ ። በምንትኑ ፣ ትትማሰልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ትት
That is what my eye has seen, and what my ear has heard. And I know as you know also; and I am not more ignorant than you. But I will speak then to God; and I will defend myself before him, if he wishes. But you, you are physicians of iniquity; and you are all comforters of evil. It does not seem just to me that I listen to you, because wisdom has disappeared from you. Listen to the defense of my mouth; and hear from me the judgment of my lips. Now also will you not speak before God, and manifest deceit before him? And will you move away from him?
Judge then for yourselves. But it is good that he examines you; when you do everything, do you procure it? In what do you compare yourselves to him so that you plead with him?


[p. 607] {#po.2.p607}
ዋቀሥዎ ። ለገጽሰ ፡ ታደልዉ ፡ ወለፌ ፡ ተኅብኡ ። ”ቀዳሚሁ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ኃይሉ ፡ ያፈርሀ ክሙ ። ወይመጽአክሙ ፡ ግሩም ፡ እምኅቤሁ ። ”ዘለፋክሙሂ ፡ ኀላፊ ፡ከመ ፡ ሐመድ። ወሥጋሂ ፡ መሬት ። ”ተጸመሙ ፡ ወእንብብ ። ወእሥኀት ፣ እምሕምዝ ። "ወአስተጋብ እ ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ በስነኒየ ። ወአጥቃ ፡ ለነፍስየ ፡ በአዴየ ። ”ወእመሰ ፡ እትመጠዋ ፡ ኃያል ፡ ወአማእኮሰ ፡ ተንሥተ ። ወእማአኮ ፡ እነግር ፡ ወእወቅሥ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ። "ወዝንቱ ፣ ይኩ ነኒ ፡ መድኃኒተ ። እስመ ፡ ኢይበውእ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ትምይንት ። "ስምዑኒ ፡ ስምዑኒ ፡ ቃልየ ። እንግርክሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ታጸምኡኒ ። ”ናሁ ፡ ቀረብኩ ፡ ኀበ ፣ ፍትሕየ ። አአምር አነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታስተረኢ ፡ ጽድቅየ ። ”ወመኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘትጸዐር ፡ ከማየ ። ከመ ፡ አጸመ ም ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ወአርምም ። ”ወእኩን ፡ ከመ ፡ አብድ ፡ ወአትኃባእ ፣ እምገጽከ ። "አእት
But in front you flatter, and from the side you dissimulate. First, does his strength not frighten you? And it will happen to you what is terrible. Your reproach is fleeting like ash, and your body like dust. Be silent, and I will speak; and I will rest from the fury. And I will seize my body with my teeth; and I will compress my soul in my hand. Perhaps the Powerful will take it, if not, it will be destroyed; and if not I will speak, and I will plead before him. And that will be salvation for me, because deceit does not enter before him. Listen to me, listen to my word; I will speak to you, while you listen to me. Here I am near my sentence; and I know that my justice will be manifest. And who is it who was tormented like me, so that I be mute now, and that I become silent? And so that I be like a fool, and so that I hide myself from your face? Move your hand away from me,


[p. 608] {#po.2.p608}
ት ፡ እምኔየ ፡ እዴከ ። ወኢያጥቀኒ ፡ ግርማከ ። ”ወትጴውርእኒ ፡ ወአነሂ ፡ አሰጠወከ ። ወ እመሂ ፡ ተናገርከኒ ፡ አነሂ ፡ አወሥአከ ። ”ሚመጠን ፣፡ እሙንቱ ፤ ኃጣውኢየ ፡ ወአበሳ የ ። አርኢየኒ ፡ ምንት ፡ እሙንቱ ። "ለምንት ፡ ትሴውር ፡ እምኔየ ። ወትሬሲየኒ፣ ጸላኢ ከ ። ”ወከመ ፡ ቄጽል ፡ ዘይትነገፍ ፡ እምነፋስ ፡ ታኃፍረኒ ። ወከመ ፡ ሣዕር ፡ ዘይነሥኦ ፡ ነፋስ ፣ ታሠርሐኒ ። ”እስመ ፣ ጸሐፍከ ፡ እከየ ፡ ላዕሌየ ። ወዐቀብከ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኃጢአተ ፡ ንእስየ ፡ ወአቅነትከኒ ። *” ወአቀምኮን ፡ ለእገርየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዳሕፅ ። ወሜምክ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኩ ሎ ፡ ምግባርየ ። ወሶበ ፡ እምቆመት ፡ እገርየ ፡ ኀደገኒ ። ”ወአብለይከኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝቅ ። አው ፡ ከመ ፡ ልብስ ፡ ብላዐ ፡ ቆፉንጐኔ ።
and your terror will not press me. And you will call me, and I too will listen to you; and if you speak to me, I will answer you. How many are my sins and my crimes? Show me what they are. And why do you hide yourself from me, and make me your adversary? And like the leaf, which is carried away by the wind, you snatch me; and like the herb, which the wind has taken, you torment me. Because you have inscribed my wickedness against me; and you have kept the sins of my youth, and you have enclosed me. And you have placed my feet on the slippery obstacle; and you have burdened me with all my works;
መዋቲ ፡ ዘይትወለድ ፡ እምአንስት ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ። ወምሉእ ፡ መቅሠፍተ። "ወይፈሪ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጽጌ ፡ ወይወድቅ ፡ ወይኀልፍ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጽላሎት ። ወኢይሄሉ ፡ እንከ ።
and if my foot placed itself, you would have left me. And you have consumed me with old age like a wineskin, or like a garment, food for the moth. The mortal, who is born of woman, his days are short, and his torment is complete; and he flourishes like the flower, and falls; and he passes like the shadow, and remains no more.


[p. 609] {#po.2.p609}
"ወዝኒ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ሐተተከ ። ወሎቱኒ ፡ አባእኮ ፡ ቅድሜከ ፣ ይትዋቅሥ ። "መኑ ፡ ይነጽ ሕ ፡እምርስሐት ፡ወኢመኑሂ ። "ወዘአሐተ ፡ ፅለተ ፡ ሐይወ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ። እስመ፣ ጐሉቅ ፣ አውራጊሁኒ ፡ በኅቤሁ ። ወትሁቦ ፡ ዕድሜሁ ፡ ወኢይኀልፍ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ”ረሐ ቅ ፡እምኔሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርፍ ። ወይትዐቀባ ፡ ለሕይወቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዐሳብ ። "ዕፅኒ ፡ ቦ፡ተስ ፋ። ተገዚሞሂ ፡ ይደግም ፡ ይሠርጽ ። ወኅይሉኒ ፡ ኢይኅልቅ ። ”ወአመሂ ፣፡ ረሥአ ፡በ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ሥርዉ ። ወእመሂ ፡ ሞተ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ አዕጹቂሁ ። ”ወአመ ዐዛ ፡ ይሠርጽ ። ወይፈሪ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ተክል ። ”ወሰብእሰ፣፡ እምከመ ፡ ሞተ ፡ይጸይ እ። ወእምከመ ፡ሞተ ፡ መዋቲ፡፣ ኢይሄሉ ፡ እንከ ። " በሕርኒ ፡ ቦ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቀብል ። ወፈ ለግኒ ፡ ይነጽፍ ፣ ወይየብስ ። "”ወሰብአሰ ፡ እምከመ ፡ ኖመ ፡ ኢይነቅህ ፡ እንከ ። እስከ፣
And that one, has he not scrutinized you, and [have] you not made him enter before you so that he may defend himself? And who is pure of stain? No one. Not even he who has lived a single day on the earth, because the number of his months is not his; and you give him his term, and he does not cross it. Move away from him, so that he may rest, so that he may wait for his life, like a mercenary. The tree also has hope; and, having been cut, again it flourishes and sprouts; and its strength is not extinguished; and if its root has aged in the earth, and if its branches have died on the rock; it sprouts by the scent of water, and it gives fruit, like a new plant. But man, from his death, falls into rot; and since the mortal is dead, he exists no more.
The lake also has its hope, when it empties; and the river also, when it dries up, and withers. But man, since he is asleep, does not wake up anymore,


[p. 610] {#po.2.p610}
አመ ፡ ይትረኀው ፡ ሰማይ ። ወይነቅሁ ፡ እምንዋሞሙ ። ”ወእምኅየሰኒ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ ዐቀብከኒ ። ወእምኃባእከኒ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይዬርር ፡ መዐትከ ። ወአምዐደምክኒ ፡ እ ስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ትዜከረኒ ። ”ሶቤሁ ፡ የሐዩ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሞተ ። ፈጺሞ ፡ መዋፅ ለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ። እምተዐገስኩ ፡ እስከ ፡ እትወለድ ፡ ዳግመ ። ”ወአምአውሣአኩከ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ጸዋፅከኒ ። ወኢትመንን ፡ ተግባረ ፡ እዴከ ። ”ኀለቄኑ ፣ ኅጣውኢየ ። አሐተኒ ፡ አልቦ ኑ ፡ ዘረሳዕከ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አበሳየ ። ”"ኃተምክኑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቀ፡ናመት ፡ ጌጋይየ ። ወኩሎ ፡ ዘአበስኩ ፡ ዘላዕሌየ ። "ከመ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዘንሕለ ፡ ወወድቀ ። ወኩኩሕኒ ፣ ይበሊ ፡ በ ውስተ ፡ ምንባሩ ። ”ወአብንሂ ፡ ይለምጽ ፡ እማይ ። ወማያደኒ ፡ ይደፍን ፣ በማዕበል ፡ ምድረ ፡ ልዑለ ። ወአሕጐልከ ፡ ትዕግስቶ ፡ ለሰብእ ። "ወታአቶ ፡ ለዝሉፉ ፡ ወይማስ
until the sky passes, and he wakes from his sleep. And it would be better for me, if you kept me in the tomb, and if you hid me until your anger had passed and if you reserved me until you had remembered me. If man had to live, after his death, having accomplished the days of his life, I would be patient, until I had been born again. And I would answer you, when you called me; and you would not despise the work of your hand. Have you counted my sins? Have you forgotten none of my sins and my crimes? Have you sealed my errors in a bag, and everything that I have committed of evil, which is to my charge? Like the mountain, which has subsided, and has fallen; and the rock also ages in its place. And the stone also is polished by water; and the water in its turn hides in the midst of the high earth; and you have annihilated the patience of man. And you move away forever, and he corrupts; and you have turned your face toward him, and you have snatched him.


[p. 611] {#po.2.p611}
ን ። ወሜጥከ ፡ ገጸከ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወመሠጥከ ። "አመሂ ፡ በዝጐ ፡ ደቂቁ ፣ ኢይሬኢዮ ሙ። ወአመሂ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ ኢያአምሮሙ ። 'ዳአሙ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ማሰነ ፡ ወነፍሱሂ ፡ ለ ሕይወት። ወተሰጥወ ፡ እሊፈዝ ፡ ቴምናዊ ፡ ወይቤ ። "ቀዳሚሁ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ወያወሥአ ፡ ዘይ ሔሊ ፡ መንፈስ ። ወአጽገብካ ፡ ጻዕረ ፡ ለነፍስነ ፡ ወከርሥነ ። ”ወትወቅሥ ፡፥ነገረ ፡ ከ ንቱ ። ወትነብብ ፡ ዘኢይበቀዕዕ ። ”አኮሁ ፡ ለሊከ ፡ ኢፈራህክ ። ከመ ፡ ትንብብ ፡ ዘከመ ዝ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ”መጠንከ ፡ አምቃለ ፡ አፉከ ። ወኢፈለጥከ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ዐበይተ ። "ይዘልፈከ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አፉከ፡፣ ወአኮ ፡ አነ ። ወከናፍሪከ ፡ መሐተትከ። ቦኑ ፡ እምሰብእ ፣፤ ቀደምከ ፡ ተፈጥሮ ። አው ፣ ከመ ፡ አስሐትያኑ ፡ ጠጋእከ ። "ወት አዛዘ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔርኑ ፡ ሰማዕከ ። ወበኀቤከኑ ፣ ኅልቀት ፡ ጥበብ ። "ወምንተ ፣ ታ
And if his sons are numerous, he will not see them; and if they are few, he will not know them. But his body will corrupt, as well as his soul for life. And Eliphaz of Teman answered, and said: Its beginning is wisdom, and he will answer what the spirit meditates; and you have satiated our soul and our belly with torment. And you argue with vain discourses; and you say what is not useful. Is it that you yourself do not have fear of uttering such a discourse before God? You are evaluated by the word of your mouth; and you have not distinguished the discourse of the great. And the word of your mouth will rebuke you, and not me; and your lips will be your witness. Have you been created the first of men? Or have you been rendered compact like ice?
Have you heard the orders of God? Is wisdom limited to you? And what do you know that we do not know?


[p. 612] {#po.2.p612}
አምር ፡ ዘኢናአምር ። ወምንተ ፡ ትሌቡ ፡ ዘኢንሌቡ ። "ወልሂቀኒ ፡ ወአአሩገኒ ፡ ቦኀ ቤነ ። ዘይረሥአ ፡ እምአቡከ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ። ”ወእመሂ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፣፡ አበስከ ፡ ተቀሠፍከ ። ኢኮነ ፡ ዝነገር ፡ ዘነበብከ ፡ በአምጣንከ ። "ምንተ ፡ ኃበለ ፡ ልብከ ። ወምንተ ፡ ርኢያ ፡ አፅይንቲከ ። "ከመ ፡ትትባሣፅዕ ፡ በመዐት ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ። ወዘከመዝ ፡ ነገ ረ ፡ ታውፅአእ ፡ እምአፉከ ። ”መኑ ፡ መዋቲ ፡ ዘይነጽሕ ። ወመኑ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ዘይትወለድ ፡ እምአንስት ። ”ለቅዱሳን ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ኢይትአመኖሙ ። ወሰማይኒ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ በቅድ ሜሁ ።፡ ”ኅድግሰ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ወርሱሕ ። ዘይሠርባ ፡ ለኃጢአት ፡ ከመ ፡ ስታይ ። ስምዐኒ ፡ እንግርከ ። ወአይድዕከ ፡ ዘርኢኩ ። ”ዘነገሩ ፡ ጠቢባን ። ወኢኀብኡ ፡ አበዊ ሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለባበሕቲቶሙ ፡ ተውህበት ፡ ምድር ። ወኢኅለፈ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እ ምኔሆሙ ።፡ ”ዙሉ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለኃጥእ ፣ ይትሐዘብ ። ወዓመቲሀኒ ፡ በኀልቀ፡ ፡ ይት
And what have you understood that we do not understand? The young man, and the old man, and the elders are among us, and who is more advanced in his days than your father? Although you have sinned but a few times, you have been chastised; is this not the discourse you have spoken according to your power? What has your heart dared, and what have your eyes seen, that you argue with anger before God, and that you let such words come out of your mouth? What mortal is pure, and what just man is born of woman? He hardly trusts in the saints; and even the heaven has not been pure before him; how much more the impure and defiled man, who drinks sin like a beverage. Listen to me; I will tell you, and I will announce to you what I have seen; what the wise have reported;
and the fathers will hide nothing from their sons. Because the earth was given to them alone; and no other people surpassed them. All the days of the sinner are burdened with worries;


[p. 613] {#po.2.p613}
ወሀብ ፡ ለኃያል ። ”ወትግርምቱሂ ፡ ያወድቆ ። አመ ፡ ተሰፈወ ፡ ዳኅነ። ይንበር ፡ ይትገፈ ታአ ። ”ወአልቦ ፡ ተስፋ ፡ ወዒኦ ፡ አምጽልመት ። ወተአዘዘ ፡ ወድአ ፡ ውስተ ፡እደ ፡ ኀጺን ። ”ወተውህሀበ ፡ መብልዐ ፡ ለአንስርት ። ወያአምር ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ድቀት ። ወት ነፅኖ ፡ ፅለተ ፡ ጽልመት ። ”ወያጸድፎ ፡ ሕማም ፡ ወምንዳቤ ። ወከመ ፡ መልአክ ፡ዘይቀ ድም ፡ ተሠይሞ ፡ ወይጎስር ። "እስመ ፡ አዕበየ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር። ወአግዘፈ ፡ ክሳዶ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ዘኩሎ ፣ ይመልክ ። "ወትረውጽ ፡ ኅሳ ሩ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ። ወውስተ ፡ ረሕበ ፡ ዘባኑ ፡ ኃይሉ ። ”ወይከድኖ ፡ ለገጹ ፡ ሐሞቱ ። ወ ጠበቆ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐወሁ ። ”ወይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ሐቅል ። ወይበውእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያት ፡ መዝብር ። ወዘእአሙንቱ ፡ አስተዳለዉ ፡ ባዕድ ፡ ይበልዖ ። ”ወኢይብዕሉሂ ፡
and his days and his years are given in count to the valiant. And his threats will make him fall; when he had the hope of being safe, he will be overturned. And there is no hope for him to escape from the darkness, and he has already been delivered into an iron hand. And he has been given as food to the eagles; and he knows himself that there is a ruin and that the day of chastisement and darkness awaits him. And pain and anguish will precipitate him into the abyss; and like a prince who places himself in front, and is humiliated; because he has raised his hands before God, and he has fattened his neck in the presence of God, Almighty. And his ignominy runs before him; and his strength is in the breadth of his back. And his gall will hide his face; and it is attached to his loins. And he dwells in the cities of the desert; and he enters into ruined houses; and what they have prepared, another will eat. And they will not be rich, and their riches will not last;


[p. 614] {#po.2.p614}
ወኢይገዲ ፡ ንዋዮሙ ። ወኢይገብር ፡ ጽላሎተ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ። ”ወኢያመስጥ ፡ እ ምጽልመት ። ለከርሠሂ ፡ ያየብሶ ፡ ነፋስ ። ወይወድቅ ፡ ፍሬሁኒ ። ”ወኢይሴፎ ፡ ሕይወ ተ። ወዘተሰፈወሂ ፡ ያመስጦ ። "ወይጠፍእ ፡ ትውክልቱሂ ፡ በጊዜሁ ። ወኀይሉሂ ፡ ኢ ይጸንዕ ። ”ወይትበላዕ ፡ ቁዑ ፡ በምዕር ። ወይወድቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ዘይትነገፍ ። "መሐ ተቶሙ ፡ ለኃጥአን ፡ ሞት ። አሳት ፡ ያውዒ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እለ ፡ ይነሥኡ ፡ ሕልያነ ። "ይፀን ሱ ፡ በከርሦሙ ፡ጻዕረ ። ወትመልእ ፡ ከርምሙ ፡ ጐሕሉተ።
and he will not give shade on the earth. And he will not escape from the darkness; and the wind will dry up his belly; and his fruit will fall. And he will not have the hope of life; and what he has waited for will escape him. And his assurance disappears in its time: and his strength is not firm at all. And his grape, still green, is eaten in a moment; and it falls like the fruit that is rejected. Death is the seal of sinners;
ወተሰጥወ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ወይቤ ። “ብዙኀ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ዘከመዝ ። መናዝዛነ ፡ ለእኪት ፡ ዙልክሙ ። ”ዙሎ ፡ ዘመጻእክሙ ፡ ትንብቡ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዐቅም ። ወምንተ ፡ አጽኅብኩ ክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትዋሥኡኒ ። ” አነሂ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ትብሉ ፡ አወሥአክሙ ። በነፍስክሙ ፣ት
the fire burns the house of those who have received the gift of iniquity. They have conceived torment in their belly; and their belly is filled with deceit. And Iyob answered, and said: I have heard many such things; and you are all miserable comforters. You say without measure everything that has happened to you; and why should I importune you so that you argue with me? And I too will answer you in the way you have spoken. Do you redeem my soul with your soul?


[p. 615] {#po.2.p615}
ቤዝውዋ ፡ ለነፍስየ ። "አነግረክሙ ፡፥ነገረ ። ወአኀውሥ ፡ ርእስየ ፡ ለክሙ ። "ሶቤሁ፣ብ የ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ውስተ ፣ አፉየ ። ወኢምሕኩ ፡ከናፍርየ ፡ አምአኀሥኩ ። "እመሂ ፡ነበብኩ ፡ ኢይጤዒ ፡ ቀስልየ ። ወእመሂ ፡ አርመምኩ ፡ ይብእሰኒ ፡ ሕማምየ ። "ይእዜሰ ፡ ግመዳ ተ ፡ አብድ ። ቅንጣባተ ፡ ረሰይከኒ ። "ወኮነት ፡ ስምዐ ፡ እኅዘትየ ። ወተንሥአት ፡ ላዕሌ የ ፡ ሐሰትየ ። ወተዋሥአተኒ ፡ቅድመ ፡ገጽየ ። "ተመዒዖ ፡ በመዐት ፡ ነፅኀኒ ። ወሐቀየ ፡ ስነኒሁ ፡ ላዕሌየ ። ወአሕጻሁኒ ፡ በዲቤየ ፡ አመከረ ። ”ወአለከወኒ ፡ ወጸለለኒ ፡ አዕይንት የ ። ወብሕቁ ፡ አጽደፈኒ ፡ በብረክየ ። ወኅቡረ ፣ ሮዱኒ ። “ወመጠወኒ ፡ውስተ ፡እደ፣፤ ዘይትበሐዘኒ ። ወገደፈኒ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ለፀዋጋን ። "እንዘ ፡ ዳኅነ ፡ እነብር ፡ ገደፈ
And I will address a discourse to you; and I will shake my head at you. And if I had strength in my mouth, I would not spare my lips from doing better. And although I speak, my wound will not heal; and if I am silent, my pain will worsen for me. But now you have made me a segment of a fool and a piece. And my arrest was a witness; my lie has risen against me; and it has accused me before my face. And getting carried away in his anger, he has overturned me; and he has gnashed his teeth against me; and he has tried his darts on me. He has darkened and covered my eyes with darkness; and in a short time he has thrown me on my knees; and all together they have run against me.
And he has delivered me into the hand of the one who was spying on me; and God has thrown me to the perverse. And when I was dwelling in safety, he has rejected me;


[p. 616] {#po.2.p616}
ኒ ። በሥዕርተ ፣ ድማኅየ ፡ መሐወኒ ። ወአስተኃይጽ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዐይን ። ”እስመ ፡ ከዋንው ፡ ወግአኒ ። ወይረግዙኒ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ዙለያትየ ፣ ወኢይምሕሩኒ ። ወተክፅወት ፡ ውስተ ፡ምድር ፡ ሐሞትየ ። ”ወነፅጉኒ ፣ ድቀተ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ድቀትየ ። ወሮጹ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ኃያላን ። ”ሠቀ ፡ አስተጣበቁ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መእስየ ። ወተሰልጠት ፡ ሕማምየ ፡ በውስተ ፡መ ሬት ። ትነድድ ፡ ከርሥየ ፡ በብካይ ። ወውስተ ፡ ቀራንብትየ ፡ ጽላሎትየ ። ”ወአልቦ ፡ ዐመፃ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደዊየ ። ወንጹሕ ፡ ጸሎትየ ። ”መሬትኑ ፡ ዳእሙ ፣ ኢይከድነኒ፣ ሥጋ የ ፡ ወደምየ ። ወኢይረክብኑ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ አአወዩ ። ”ወይእዜሂ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ሰማዕትየ ፣ ው ስተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወያአምር ፡ ሊተ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ አርያም ። ”ኅለፈት ፡ ጸሎትየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አግዚ አ ፡ብሔር ። ወታንጸፈጽፍ ፡ ዐይንየ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ። ”ሶበሁ ፡ ያስተዋቅሥዎ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ በ
and he has taken me by the hair of the nape, watching me like a spy. For he has wounded me with darts; and they have pierced me in the loins, and they have not had pity on me; and my gall has spilled on the earth. They have overturned me blow after blow; and the valiant have run against me. I have put sackcloth on my skin; and my pain has been consumed on the dust. My belly burns from tears; and the shadow of death is in my eyelids. And there is no iniquity in my hands; and my prayer is pure. Will not the dust cover my body and my blood, and will I not meet a land where I groan? And now also, behold I have my witness in heaven; and he who is in the heights knows me. My prayer has passed up to God; and my eyes will shed tears before him. If it is permitted to man to argue before God, as man with his neighbor,


[p. 617] {#po.2.p617}
ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ወለእጓለ ፡ እመ ፡ ሕያው ፡ምስለ ፡ ቢጹ ። መጽአኒ ፡ ዓመ ት ፡ ጐሉቃት ። ወአሐውር ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ኢይገብእ ። ይጹዕረኒ ፡ ዘያወፅኣ ፡ ለነፍስየ ። እትሜነዮ ፡ ለመቃብር ፡ ወኢያደምዖ ። "”አልህሀስ ፡ ሠራሕኩ ፡ ወምንተ፤እረሲ ። "ወሰረቁኒ ፡ ነኪራን ፡ንዋይየ ። ወመኑ ፡ ውአቱዝ ፡ ዘይም ቅሕዎ ፡ በእዴየ ። "እስመ ፣ ኅኀብእዎ ፣ ለልቦሙ ፣ እምሠናይ ። በእንተዝ ፣ ኢያሌዕሎ ሙ ፡፡ ”ወይሜህርዎ ፡ እከየ ፡ ለልቦሙ ። ወተመሰወኗ ፡ አዕይንትየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ደቂቅየ ። ረሰይከኒ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ። ወኮንክዎሙ ፡ ሠሐቀ ፡ ወስላቀ ። "' ወዐውረኒ ፡ አዕይንት የ ፡እአምአንብፅ ። ወፈድፋደ ፣ አሥቆረረኒ ፡ ኩሉ ። ”ወተአምንዋ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ወቀብፁኒ ። ወርቱዕሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኃጥአን ፡ትግባእ ። ”ወባሕቱ ፡ እትአመን ፡ ከመ ፡ እገብአ ፡ ለፍኖትየ ።
many years will come for me, and I will walk by a path from which I will not return. He who brings out my soul torments me; I desire the sepulcher, but I do not obtain it. I disappear, I have exhausted myself; what shall I do? And strangers have stolen my goods; and who is it who will return them into my hand? Because they have hidden their heart far from the good; and for that, they have not raised them. And they teach evil to their heart; and my eyes have melted because of my children. You have made me the fable of the nations; and I was for them an object of derision and a toy. And my eyes have blinded because of tears; and all have hated me strongly.
I had trusted in this, and they have abandoned me; and the upright man has turned against the sinners. But I trust that I will return by my way;


[p. 618] {#po.2.p618}
ወእስመ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ እደዊየ ፡ አረክባ ፡ ለትፍሥሕትየ ። ”ወሀለውክሙ ፡ትርአዩ ፡ ዙልክ ሙ ፡ ወትምጽኡኤ ። አስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ርትዐ ፡ ዘአረክብ ፡ በኅቤክሙ ።፡ ወኃልቃ ፡ መዋፅ ልየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ጽዩአት ። ወተበትከ ፡ ሥርወ ፡ ልብየ ። "ሞዐልት ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ኮነኒ ። ወጽ ልመት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ገብአኒ ። ”ወእመሂ ፣ ጐንደይኩ ፡ መቃብር ፡ ዳአሙ ፤፡ ቤትየ ። ወውስተ ፡ ጽልመት ፡ተነጽፈ ፡ ምስካብየ ። " ለሞትሂ ፡ አቡየ ፡ አቤሎ ። ወእምየ ሂ ፡ ወአኃዊየ ፡ ፅጸያት ። "አይቴ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እንከ ፡ ተስፋየ ። እሬኢያኑ ፡ዓዲ ፡ ለሠናይ ትየ ። ”ወትወርድኑ ፡ምስሌየ ፡ውስተ ፡ መቃብር። ወንደፈኑ ፡ኅቡረ ፡ ውስተ ፡መሬት።
and because my hands are pure, I will find my joy. And you, you all have to see and you will return here; because I do not meet rectitude among you. And my days have passed exhaling a fetid odor and the nerve of my heart has been cut. The day has been night for me; and the darkness has taken the place of the light. And if I have delayed, the sepulcher alone is my house; and my bed has been spread in the darkness. To death, I say: my father: and my mother and my brothers, they are the worms. Where then is my hope?
”ወተሰጥወ ፡ በልዳዶስ ፡ ወይቤ ። አስከ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ ኢታረምም ። ተዐገስ ፡ ንንግ ርከ ፡ ንሕነሂ ። ”ለምንትኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ አርመምነ ፡ ቅድሜከ ። " ትደልወከ ፡ መቅሠ
Will I still see my good? Will it descend into the sepulcher, and will we hide ourselves together in the dust? And Baldados answered, and said: Until when will you not be silent? Wait; we will speak to you also. Why should we be silent before you like beasts? Your affliction is due to you;


[p. 619] {#po.2.p619}
ፍትከ ። ምንትኑ ፡ አንተ ፡ ለአመ ፡ ሞትክ ። ወአልቦኑ ፡ ዘይጌይስ ፡ አምኔክ ፡ በታሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ይወድቁኑ ፡ አድባር ፡ አመሰረቶሙ ።፡ " ወይጠፍአኑ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ኃጥአን ። ወኢት ትከበት ፡ አከዮሙ ። " ወብርሃኖሙ ፣ ጽልመተ ፡ ይጌግጹ ። ወማኅቶቶሙኑ ፡ ትጠፍእ ። ወይነሥኤ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ትሑታን። ወትድሕዕኑ ፡ ምክሩ ። " ወትወርድኑ ፡ አግሩ፣ ውስተ ፣ መሥገርት ። ወትትአሠርኑ ፡ በመሥገርት ። " ወይመጽኣአሁ ፡ መሣግር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወይጸ ንዑ ፡ጽሙአን ፡ላዕሌሁ ። ”ወኅቡእ ፡ ዲበ፣ ምድር ፡ ሐብሉ። ወይስሕብዎ ፡ እምፍኖቱ። "ወያጠፍኣ ፣ ሕማም ፡ እምአውዱ ። መጽኡ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ታሕተ ፡ አገሪሁ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ረኀብ ። "ወፅኑሕ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኃሳር ፡ ፅጽብት ። ”ወይበጐብኀ፡ ፡ አቀያጸ ፡ እገሪሁ ። ወ ይበልዖ ፡ ሞት ፡ ምሥናዮ ። ”ወትነቅፅ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ጥፅይት ። ወትጸንፅ ፡ ምንዳቤሁ ፡
and what will you be, if you die? And is there no one who is better than you under the heaven? Will the mountains fall from their foundations? And will the light of the sinners be extinguished, and their evil be hidden? And their light will be darkness for them, they will be frightened; and their lamp, will it be extinguished? And the humble will take their goods, and will his counsel slip? And will his foot descend into the net, and will he be taken in the net? And the nets will come upon him; and the thirsty will prevail against him. And his cord will be hidden under the earth; and they will drag him out of his path. And pain will oppress around him; and many come under his feet with great hunger. And a grave outrage threatens him. And the legs of his feet will rot; and death will devour his best part. And health will flow from his body;


[p. 620] {#po.2.p620}
ወይትቤቀሎ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ”ወይትዐጸው ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ በሌሊት ። ወይዘርዎ፣ ምሥናዮ ፡ ፈጣሪሁ ። ”ወይየብስ ፡ ሥረዊሁ ፣ በመተሕቴሁ ። ወእመልፅልቴሁ ፡ ይወድ ቅ ፡ ጽጌሁ ። ”ወይጠፍእ ፡ እምድር ፡ ዝክሩ ። ወይርሕቅ ፡ ስሙ ፡ነዋኀ። "ወያፈልስ ዎ ፡ እምብርሃን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽልመት ። ”ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይምሕሮ ፡ እምሕዝቡ ። ወኢይድኅ ን ፡ ቤቱ ፡ እምታሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወባዕድ ፡ ይዴለው ፡ በንዋየ ፡ ዚአሁ ። "”ወግዕረ ፡ ነዳ ይ ፤ በእንቲአሁ ። ወአንከርዎ ፡ ዐበይትሂ ። ”ከመዝ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ዓማዊያን ። ወዝ ውእቱ ፡ ግፅዞሙ ፡ ለአለ ፣ ኢይፈርሀዎ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ።
and his oppression will be heavy, and God will punish him. And he will lock himself in his house during the night; and his creator will disperse his best part. And his root will dry up in its lower part; and his flower will fall from its upper part. And his memory will disappear from the earth; and his name will move far away. And one will push him from the light into the darkness. And there will be no one of his people who has pity on him. And his house will not be safe under the heaven; and the stranger will enrich himself with his goods. And the poor will cry against him;
"ወተሰጥወ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ወይቤ ። ”እስከ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ ትጹዕርዋ ፡ ለነፍስየ ። ወትቀት ሉኒ ፡ በነገርክሙ ። ”አእምሩ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ረሰየኒ ። ት
and the great will be in astonishment concerning him. Such is the dwelling of unjust men; and behold the condition of those who do not fear God. And Iyob answered, and said: Until when will you torment my soul, and kill me with your discourses? Know only that God has done this to me;


[p. 621] {#po.2.p621}
ትላኩዩኒ ፡ ኢትኃፍሩ ። ትቀውሙ ፡ ላፅለ ፡ ነፍስየ ። " ኦሆ ፡ እንከ ፡ አነ ፡ አበስኩ ፡ አ ማን ።፡ ወኅቤየ ፡ ኃደረት ፡ አበድ ። ”ወነበብኩ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዘኢያአምር ። ወነገርየኒ ፡ አበድ 0፡ ወአከኮሰ ፣ ዘአዜለፍ ። "አሌ ፡ ሊተ ፡ በላዕሌየ ፡ ዳአሙ ፡ ታዐቢዩ ፣ አፉክሙ ። ወትትቄ ናደዩ ፣ ብየ ፡ ወትዘረኪዩኒ ። ”አአምሩ ፣ ዳአሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ዘሆከኒ ። ወአነኀ ፡፣ መዐቶ ፡ ላዕሌየ ። "ናሁ ፣ እሠርሕ ፡ በሒስ ፡ ወኢይነግር ። ወአአወዩ ፡ ወአ ልቦ ፡ ዘይፈትሕ ፡ ሊተ ። ”ወተሐንጸ ፡ አውድየ ፡ ወአልብየ ፡ ምኅላፈ ። ወአንጦለዐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጽየ ፡ በጽልመት ። ”ወሰለበኒ ፡ ትርሲትየ ። ወአእትት ፣ አክሊለ ፡ አምራእስ የ ። ”ወመሐወኒ እንተ ፡ ኩልሄ ፡ ወእልህስ ። ወገዘማ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕፅ ፣ ለሕይወትየ ። “ወረ ሰየኒ ፡ ለኃያል ፡ መቅሠፍት ። ወረሰየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀር ። “ወኅቡረ ፣ መጽአ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ መ
and you blame me, and you are not ashamed; and you resist my soul. If therefore I have sinned in truth; and if folly has dwelt with me; and if I have said words that I do not understand; and if my discourse has been foolish, and there is nothing for which I am blamed. Alas! you raise your mouth only against me; and you exult insolently against me, and you slander me. Know then that it is God who has troubled me; and he has raised his anger against me. Behold I am tormented by reproach, and I will not speak; and I complain, and there is no one who does me justice. And what surrounds me is closed with walls, and I have no passage; and he has veiled my face with darkness. And he has stripped me of my ornament; and he has taken the crown from my head. And he has torn me from everywhere I go, and I perish; and he has cut my life like a tree.
And he has made me a cause of affliction for the powerful: and he has made me like an enemy. And his anger has come all at once against me; and those who were spying on me have besieged my paths.


[p. 622] {#po.2.p622}
ዐቱ ። ወዐገቱኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍነዊየ ፡ እለ ፡ ይፀንሐኒ ። " ወአኃዊየኒ ፣ ተናከሩኒ ። ወአብ ደሩ ፡ ነኪረ ፡ እምኔየ ። ወአፅርክትየኒ ፡ ኢምሕሩኒ ። ”“ወአዝማድየኒ ፣ ኢያንከሩኒ ። ወእ ለሂ ፣ ያአምሩ ፡ ስምየ ፡ ረስዑኒ ። "ወአለሂ ፣ ቅሩባንየ ፡ ኢፈቀዱ ፡ ይርአዩኒ ። ወዘከ መ ፡ አምካልእ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ያመስሉኒ ። ”ወነባሪየኒ ፡ አጴውዖሙ ፣ ወይጴጹመሙኒ ። "ወ ብአሲሷትየኒ ፡ እትጋነይ ፡ ላቲ ። ወኢዬውሃ ፡ ወአስተበቀዖሙጮ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ ገዛእየ ፡ ወእት ሜሐር ፡ ሎሙ ። ”ወቀብፁኒ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወሶበሂ ፡ እትነሣእ ፡ የሐምዩኒ ። "ወአለሂ ፡ ያ አምሩኒ ፡ ያሥቆርሩኒ ። ወአለሂ ፡ አፈቅር ፡ እሙንቱ ፣ ተንሥኡ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ነፍስየ ። "ይ ትቤጸል ፡ መእስየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋየ ። ወየሐቂዩኒ ፡ አዕጽምትየ ። ”መሐሩኒ ፡ መሐሩኒ፣ አዕርክትየ ። እደ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘገሰሰኒ ። "ለምንት ፣ አንትሙኒ ፡ ታሠ ርሙኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ኢትጸግቡኑ ፡ እምሥጋየ ። ”መኑ ፡ እምጸሐፎ ፡ ለነገ
And my brothers have denied me, and they have put a stranger before me; and also my friends have not had compassion on me. And my relatives have not paid attention to me; and they have forgotten me, those who knew my name. And those who were my clients have not wanted to see me; and they have looked at me as being of another people. And I have called my servants, and they have not listened to me. And I have submitted myself to my wife, and I have caressed her; and I have begged the sons of my freedman, and I have implored them. And they have despised me forever; and when I rise, they insult me. And those who knew me, they hate me; and those who loved me have risen against my soul. My skin splits with my flesh; and my bones crack. Have pity on me, have pity on me, my friends; because it is the hand of God that has touched me. Why do you torture me like God, and are you not satiated with my flesh? Who will write my words,


[p. 623] {#po.2.p623}
ርየ ። ወይልክዖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ለዓለም ። "ውስተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ኀጺን ፡ ወዐረ ር ። ወእማእኮ ፡ ይግልፍዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩ።ኩሕ ። ”አአምር ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያገምሮ፣፣ ቤት ። ለዘ ፡ ሀለዎ ፡ ይፈውሰኒ ። "ወእንዘ ፡ ዓዲ ፣ ሀለውኩ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ይትዌለ ጥ ፡ መእስየ ። ”አምዘ ፡ ለሊየ ፡ አአምር ። ዘዐይንየ ፡ ርእየት ፡ ወአኮ ፣ ባፅድ ። ሰለጡ ኒ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕፅንየ ። ”ወእመሰ ፡ ትብሉ ፡ ምንተ ፣ ንንግር ፡ ቅድሜሁ ። ወን ርከብ ፡ ሥርወ ፡ ነገር ፡ ሎቱ ። ”ፍርሀዋ ፣ አንትሙኒ ፣ ለኃሳር ። እስመ ፣ መቅሠፍት ፡ ይመጽኦሙ ፡ ለኃጥአን ። አሜሁ ፡ ያአምሩ ፡ አይቴ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እከዮሙ ።
and record them in a book forever, in a book of iron and lead: and at least engrave them on stone! But I know that the house will not contain him, the one who will heal me. And when I am still on the earth, my skin will change. This, I myself know, what my eye has seen, and not that of another; they have paid me everything in my bosom. But if you say to me: What will we say before him? And will we meet a foundation of discourse for him? Take care yourselves of the insult,
ወተሰጥወ ፡ ሳፈር ፡ ወይቤ ። ” ኢተሐዘብኩከ ፡ ትንብብ ፣፡ ከመዝ ። ወአልቦ ፡ ዘት ጌይስ ፡ እምኔየ ፣ አእምር ። "ስምዑኒ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ትምህርትየ ። ወያውሥአኒ ፡ መንፈ
because the scourge will come to the sinners; then they will know from where all their evil comes. And Safar answered, and said: I had not supposed that you would speak thus, and you are not worth more than me to know it. Listen to me [in] the wisdom of my instruction;


[p. 624] {#po.2.p624}
ሰ ፡ ሕሊናየ ። ”ቦሁ ፡ ዘታአምር ፡ ዘከመዝ ፡ እምአሜከ ። እምአመ ፡ ተፈጥረ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዲበ ፣ ምድር ። ”ፍሥሓሆሙ ፡ ለኃጥአን ፣ ቀትል ፡ ፀቢይ ። ወሐሜቶሙ ፡ ለረሲ ዓን ፡ሐኾል ። ”አመሂ ፡ ዐርገ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ቀሩርባኑ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ደመና ፡ መሥዋፅዕቱ ። አ መ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወዳእኩ ፡ ጸናዕኩ ። አሜሃ ፡ ይትሐጐሉ ፡ ለዝሉፉ ። ወይብሉ ፡ አለ ፡ያአ ምርዎ ፡ አይቴ ፡ ሀሎ ። ”ወሠረረ ፡ ከመ ፣ ሕልም ፡ ወኢይትረከብ ። ወያስተረኢ ፡ከ መ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ዘኢይትዐወቅ ፡ ወይመስል ፡ ጽባሐ ። ”ወርኢየቶ ፡ ዐይንየ ፡ ወኢትደግ ም ፡ እንከ ። ወኢያአምር ፡ እንከ ፡ መካኖ ። ”“ወያጠፍእዎሙ ፡ ለውሉዱ ፡ ትሑታን ። ወአደዊሁኒ ፡ እሳት ፡ ይነፍኃ ። ”ወይመልእ ፡ ጻፅረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አዕጽምቲሁ ። ወይሰክብ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ሕማሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መሬት ። ”አመ ፡ጥዕመቶ ፡ እኪት ፡ውስተ ፡አፉሁ።
and the spirit of my intelligence will answer me. Do you know that it is so, from before you, and since you are, since man was created on the earth? The joy of sinners is a great combat; and the satisfaction of the impious is perdition. And if his offering has risen up to heaven, and his sacrifice has arrived up to the clouds; and if he says: Already, I was firm; then they are lost forever; and those who knew him will say: Where is he? And he has flown away like a dream, and one has not found him anymore; and he will appear like a dream of the night, which one does not understand, and he resembles the dawn. My eye has seen him, and it will not see him again; and it will not know his dwelling anymore. And the miserable will annihilate his son; and his hands will blow the fire.
And he will fill his bones with torment; and his pain will lie down in the dust. Because the evil will be sweet in his mouth.


[p. 625] {#po.2.p625}
ወይኃብእ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ልሳኑ ። ”ወያስተጋብኦ ፡ መእከለ ፡ ኾርዔሁ ። ”“ወኢትክል ፡ ትር ድኦ ። ሕምዘ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ምድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሥ ። "” ብዕል ፡ ዘይዘገብ ፡ በዓመፃ ፡ ይደመ ሰስ ። ወያቴክሎ ፡ መልአክ ፡ እምቤቱ ። "ወይነሥኦ ፡ ሕምዘ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ምድር ። ወይቀ ትሎ ፡ ልሳነ ፡ አፍዖት ። ”ወኢይሬኢ ፡ አኾጐለ ፡ መራፅይሁ ። ወኢይፀምድ ፡ ምዕረ ፡ ወ ካፅበ ። "ለከንቱ ፡ ወለበክ ፡ ሠርሐ ። ብዕሎ ፡ ዘኢይጥዕም ፡ እምኔሁ ። ከመ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ዘ ኢይትሐየክ ፡ ወኢይወኅጥ ። ”እስመ ፡ አንሐለ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ድኩማን ። ወበር በረ ፡ መሐፍደ ፡ ወኢያግብኦ ። ”ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይሣሀሎ ፡ ለንዋዩ ። ወኢያደምፅ ፡ ፍትወ ቶ ። ”ወአልቦ ፡ ትራፈ ፡ ለብዕሉ ። በእንተዝ ፡ ኢትሠምር ፡ ሠናይቱ ። "አመ ፡ ይቤ፣
and he will hide [it] under his tongue. And he will bring [it] back to the middle of his throat; and he will not be able to help him. The venom of the serpent is in his belly. The wealth, which he has gathered by iniquity, will be destroyed; and the angel will chase him from his house. And the venom of the serpent will take him; and the tongue of the vipers will kill him. And he will not see the little ones of his flocks; and he will not harness either once or twice. He has worked for vanity and for nothingness. His wealth, of which he does not taste at all, is like a hard dish that one does not chew, nor swallow at all. Because he has overturned many houses of the weak, and he has pillaged the palace, and he does not restore. And there is no one who sympathizes with him because of his goods; and he will not obtain what he covets. And there will remain nothing of his wealth; and for that his good will not be abundant. And if he says: I am healthy, then he will be tormented; and all the pain will arrive to him,


[p. 626] {#po.2.p626}
ሰለጥኩ ፣ አሜሁ ፣ ይትመነደብ ። ወይመጽኦ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሕማም ። ዘያጸግባ ፡ ለከርሠ ። ወይትዌሰክ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ መዐት ። ወይተግህ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዘያሐሞ ። ”ወኢያመስጥ ፡ እ ምእደ ፣ ኩናት ። ወይበረብሮ ፡ ቀስተ ፡ ብርት ። ”ወየኅልፍ ፡ ሐጽ ፡ እንተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ። ወያንሶሱ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ነፍሱ ። ወላዕሌሁ ፡ ዘያደነግዖ ። "”ወይፀንሖ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ጽል መት ። ወይበልዖ ፡ እሳት ፣ ኢያንደዱ ። ወይትጋየጾ ፡ ጎሩ ፡ ለቤቱ ። ”ወይከሥቶ ፡ ሰማ ይ ፡ ጌጋዮ ። ወምድርኒ ፡ ኢትነሥኦ ። ወሞት ፡ ይስሕባ ፡ ለቤቱ ፡ ለዘላፎ ። ወትመ ጽኦ ፣ ፅለተ ፡ መንሱት ። ”ዝውአቱ ፡ መክፈልቱ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ኃጥእ ፣ እምኅበ ፣ እግዚ አ ፣ብሔር ። ወንዋዮሂ ፡ ዘአጥረየ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ፈጣሪሁ ።
which will satiate his belly; and the scourge of anger will be added to it; and he who makes him suffer will watch over him. And he will not escape from the hand of the lance; and the bow of bronze will pierce him. And the arrow will pass into his body; and the evil will walk in his soul; and upon him will be he who troubles him. And all the darkness will spy on him; and a fire that one does not light will devour him; and his neighbor will cause everything to his house. And the heaven will discover his fault; and the earth also will not receive him. And death will drag his house forever;
ወተሰጥወ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ወይቤ ። ”ስምዑኒ ፡ ወአጽምኡኒ ፡ ነገርየ ። ከመ ፡ ኢትበ
and the day of the trial will reach him. Such is the share that God has made to the sinful man; and such are the goods that he has acquired from his Creator. XXI, And Iyob answered, and said: Hear me, and listen to my words; so that you do not say: We are comforting him.


[p. 627] {#po.2.p627}
ሉ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ንናዝዞ ። ”“አርምሙ ፤ እንግርክሙ ። እመቦ ፡ ዘትሥሕቁኒ ። "ዘአኮ ፡ሰብ እ ፡ ዘይዘልፈኒ ። ወእፎ ፡ ዘኢይትመዓዕ ። ”ትኔጽሩኒ ፡ ወታነክሩ። ወትትመላትሑ ፡ በ እደዊክሙ ። "እመሂ ፡ ተዘከርኩ ፡ ተዘለፍኩ ። ኅዱር ፣ ውስተ ፣ ሥጋየ ፡ ጻዕር ። "ለም ንት ፡ኃጥአን ፡ የሐይዉ ። ወይረሥኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብፅሎሙ ። "እመሂ ፡ ዘርኡ ፣ ያሰም ሩ ። ወውሉዶሙኒ ፡ ይመልእ ፡ ዐይነ ። ” ወአብያቲሆሙኒ ፡ ምሉእ ።፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘያደነግ ፆሙ። ወኢይመጽኦሙ ፡ መቅሠፍት ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ”"ላሀሞሙኒ ፣ኢ ይመክና ። ፅኑሳቲሆሙኒ ፣፤ ኢይዳህፃ ። ወይድኅና ፡ በኅርሶን ። ” ወይዜውሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ አ ባግዕ ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወደቂቆሙኒ ፡ ይትዌነዩ ። ”ወየዐነዝሩ ፣ ወይሰነቅዉ ። ወይትፌሥ ሑ ፡ በቃለ ፡ ማኅሌቶሙ ። ”ወፈጸሙ ፣ መዋፅሊሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌግዑ ። ወውስ
Be silent, I will speak to you; unless you are mocking me. For it is not a man who reproves me: and how is it that I would not be irritated? Look, and be astonished; and place your hands on your face. And when I remembered, I was troubled; and the torment has inhabited my body. Why do sinners live, and grow old in their riches? And if they sow, they prosper before them; and likewise their sons fill the view. And their houses are full, and there is no one who frightens them; and no chastisement comes to them from God. And their cows are not sterile; and those that have conceived do not abort; and they are saved in their delivery. And they will persist like sheep forever;
and also their sons take their frolics. And they will play the harp and the cithara; and they will rejoice at the sound of their songs. And having consumed their days in pleasures,


[p. 628] {#po.2.p628}
ተ ፡ ምስካበ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ኖሙ ። "ወይቤሎ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ረሐቅ ፡ እምኔየ ። ኢይፈ ቅድ ፡ አርአይ ፡ ፍኖተከ ። ”ምንተ ፡ ይክል ፡ ከመ ፡ ንትቀነይ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወምንተ ፡ ይበ ጐዐነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሖር ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ”ውስተ ፡ እዴሆሙ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ሠናይቶሙ ። ወኢይኔ ጽሮሙ ፣ ምግባሮሙ ፡ ለኃጥአን ። ”ወትጠፍእ ፡ ማኅበሮሙ ፡ ለረሲዓን ። ወትበጽሖ ሙ ፡ ዕልወት ። ወይመጽኦሙ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወመቅሠፍቶሙ ፡፡ ”ወይከውኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሐሠ ር ፡ቅድመ፡ነፋስ ። ወከመ ፡ ጸበል ፡ ዘይነሥኦ ፡ ዓውሎ ። "ወኢይረክብዎ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ደ ቂቁ ። ወይትቤቀሎ ፡ ወያአምር ፡ ሶቤሃ ። "ወይሬኢያ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሬግዝዎ ሙ ። ወእግዚአ ፡ ብሔርኒ ፡ ኢያድኅኖሙ ። "እስመ ፡ እንተ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ይገብር ፡ በቤ ቱ ። ወቀበለ፣ አምጐልቄ ፣ አውራጊሁ ። ”ቀዳሚሁ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ እግዚአ ፣፡ ብሔር ። ውእ
