ሐዱ ፡ምስለ ፡ ክልኡ ፡ ተጣበቀ ። ወመንፈስሂ ፡ ኢይበሮ ። "ወያስተለክዕ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አጉሁ ። ወይጣበቁ ፡ ወኢይትፋትሑ ። "በዕጣሱ ፡ ይበርቅ ፡ ብርሃን ። ወአ ዕፅይንቲሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኮከበ ፡ ጽባሕ ። "ወአምአፉሁ ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ መኃትው ፡ ዘይነድድ ። ወይዌሩ ፡ አፍሐመ ፡ እሳት ። "ወእምአንፉ ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ ጢስ ። ከመ ፡ አቶን ፡ ዘይነድ ድ ፡ በአፍሐመ ፡ አሳት ። "ነፍሱሂ ፡ ፍሕም ፡ ይእቲ ። ነበልባል ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ እምአፉ ሁ። ውስተ ፡ ክሳዱ ፡ ኃደረ ፡ ኃይል ። ወውዐለ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ሞት ። " ሥጋሁ ፡ ነፍሰ ቶ ፡ ጡቡቅ ። ወለእመሂ ፡ ሶጡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ኢይትኀወሥ ። " ልቡኒ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ጾላፅ ። ቆመ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ኃያል ። " ወለአመሂ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ይሜምዕ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አራ ዊት ፡ ወእንስሳ ። ወዘ ፡ይትኀወሥ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ። "ወለአመሂ ፡ ጠበቆ ፡ ከ።ያንው ፡
And as a man attaches himself to his brother; and they are bound, and they will not be untied. The light shines in his sneezing; and his eyes are like the morning star. And from his mouth comes a lamp that burns, and that throws burning coals. And from his nostrils comes smoke, like a furnace that burns with burning coals. His breath is like embers; and the flame comes out of his mouth. Strength dwells in his neck; and death remains before him, for him who sees him. His body is bound to his spirit; and if one throws (a stone) at him, he does not stir. And his heart is firm and like the stone of a rock; and he has risen like a valiant young man. And when he turns, all the beasts and animals fear him, and everything that moves on the earth. And if spears attach themselves to him, they do nothing to him.


[p. 684] {#po.2.p684}
አልቦ ፡ ዘይሬስዮ ። ”ረምሕ ፡ ወልብሰ ፡ ኀጺን ፡ ከመ ፡ ሣዕር ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ በኀቤሁ ። ወብርትኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፅፅ ፡ ብኅቡኅ ። "ኢይሰሞሮ ፡ ቀስተ ፡ ብርት ። ወአብንሂ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሐሠር ። ”ብርዐ ፡ ይመስሎ ፡ መፍጽሕት ። ወይሥሕቅ ፡ ዲበ ፡ እብን ፡ ዓበይት ። "ም ስካቡሂ ፡ ሐውልት ፡ ዐበይት ። ወኩሉ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ፅቡር ፡ዘአ ልቦ ፤ ጐልቄ ። ወያንሥዐሥዓ ፡ ለሲኦል ፡ ከመ ፡ ጽሀርት ። ባሕርኒ ፡ በድወ ፡ ይመስ ሎ ። ”ወሞረ ፡ ሲኦልኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፄዋ ። ወረሰያ ፡ ለሲኦል ፡ ከመ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ያንሶሱ ። "ወአ ልቦ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወኢምንትሂ ፡ ከማሁ ። ተፈጢሮ ፡ ተሳለቁ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መላእክ ት ። ”ወይሬኢ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ነዋኀ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ንጉሦሙ- ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ።
The javelin and the iron breastplate are like grass before him; and the bronze is like rotten wood. And the bow of bronze does not pierce him; and the stone is like straw. The hammer seems to him like stubble; and he laughs at immense stones. His bed is like that of a great column, and all the earth of gold is for him like mud, which is not calculated. And he makes the Siol boil like a cauldron; and the sea seems to him like a desert. And the cold of the Siol is like salt and he makes of the Siol a place where one walks. And there is no one on earth who is like him; and when he was created, the angels laughed at him.
ወተሰጥዎ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ፡ ወይቤ ። " አአምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ትክ
And he sees everything that is high; and he is the king of all those who are in heaven. And Iob answered God, and said: I know that you can do everything, and that everything is possible for you;


[p. 685] {#po.2.p685}
ል ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይሰአነከ ። ”መኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘየኀብአከ ፡ ምክረ ፡ ወይምሕክ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘይከብት ፡ ይመስሎ ። ወመኑ ፡ ይነግረኒ ፣፡ ዘኢያአምር ፡ ዓቢያተ ፡ ወመንክ ረ ፡ ዘኢርኢኩ ። "ስምዐኒ ፡ አግዚኦ ፡ አነሂ ፡ እንግርከ ። ወእሴአለከ ፡ ወአንተ ፡ መህ ረኒ ። "ሰሚዐሰ ፡ ቀዲሙሂ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ በእዝንየ ። ወይእዜሰ ፣ በዐይንየ ፡ ርኢኩ ። "ወ በሕቱ ፡ በዘ ፡ ተዛለፍኩ ፡ ተመሰውኩ ። አአምር ፡ ረእስየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሐመድ ፡አነ፣ወ መሬት ።
and that there is nothing that you cannot do. Who is it that hides the counsel, and who spares words, he resembles the one who hides himself from him? And who will speak to me of the greatness that I do not know, and of the wonders that I have not seen? Listen to me, Lord, I will speak to you: and I will question you, and you will instruct me. By hearing, therefore, I have heard you with my ears, and now I have seen with my eyes. That is why I have repented and I have become discouraged;
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተናገሮ ፡ እግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ፡ ለኢዮብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ፡፤ ለኤልፋዝ ፣ ቴምናዊ ። አበስከ ፣ አንተ ፡ ወክልኤቱ ፡ አዕርክቲከ ፡ አስመ ፣ ኢተናገርክሙ ፡ ጽድቀ ፡ ቅድሜየ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ቀ-ልዔየ ፣ ኢዮብ ፣ ወኢአሐተ ሂ ። "ወይእዜሂ ፡ ንሥኡ ፡ ሰብዐተ ፡ አልህምተ ፡ ወሰብዐተ ፡ አባግዐ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ኀ
and I know myself that I am ash and dust. And after God had said these words to Iob, God said to Elephaz of Teman: You have sinned yourself and your two friends, because you have not spoken the truth before me, like Iob my servant, and no one other than him. And now take seven bulls and seven rams, and go find Iob my servant, and he will make a sacrifice for you; and Iob my servant


[p. 686] {#po.2.p686}
በ ፣፡ ሦልዔየ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ወይገብር ፡ ለክሙ ፣ መሥዋዕተ ። ወኢዮብ ፡ ቀ፡ልዔየ ፡ይጹ ሊ ፡ በእንቲአክሙ ፡ እምገጸ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ኢነሣአኩ ።፡ ወሶበ ፡ አኮ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ እምቀ ተልኩክሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢነበብክሙ ፡ ርትዐ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቀሩልዔየ ፣፡ ኢዮብ ። "ወሖሩ ፡ ኤ ልፋዝ ፡ ቴምናዊ ፣ ወበልዳዶይስ ፡ አውኬናዊ ፡፣ ወሳፍር ፡ አሜናዊ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡-እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ። ወሰረየ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ኀጢአቶሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኢየብ ። ”ወ አሕየዎ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ለኢዮብ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አዕርክቲሁ ፡ ወኀደገ ፡ ሎ ሙ ፡ ኃጢአቶሙ ጦ።ዖ ወወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ለኢየብ ፡ ካዕበተ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ንዋዩ ፡ ዘ ቀዲሙ ፡ ወአመክዐበ ፡፣ ሎቱ ። ወሰምዑ ፡ ዙሎሙ ፡ አኃዊሁ ፡ ወአኃቲሁ ፡፣ ዘረከ ቦ፣ወመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወዙሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ያአምርዎ ፡ ቀዲሙ-። ወበልዑ ፡ ወሰቲዩ ፡ም ስሌሁ ፣ ወአስተፈሥሕዎ ፡ ወአንከሩ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ ዘአምጽአ ፡ እግዚአ ፣፡ ብሔር፣፤ ላዕሌሁ ። ወወሀብዎ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለለ ፡፣ አሐዱ ፡ ፅምደ ፡ ወወርቀ ፡ ወብሩረ ፡ በመድሎት ፣ ዘደርከም ፡ አርባዕተ ። "ወባረኮ ፡ እግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ፡ ለኢዮብ ፡ ደኃሪቶ ፡ እምቀዳሚ ቱ ። ወአንስሳሁኒ ፡ እልፍ ፡ ወአርባዕቱ ፡ ምእት ፡ አባግዒሁ ፡ ”[ወአግማሊሁ ፡ ዓሠር ቱ ፡ምአት ወፅምደ ፡ አልህምቲሁ ፡ ዓሠርቱ ፡ ምአት ። ወአእዱጊሁሀኒ ፡ አእኑስ ፡ ዐ
will pray for you; I have not turned away from his face; and if it were not for him, I would kill you, because you have not spoken with righteousness against Iob my servant. And Elephaz of Teman, and Baldados of Aouken, and Safer of Amen went, and did as God had ordered them; and he forgave them their sins because of Iob. And God gave life to Iob; and Iob prayed for all his friends, and he forgave them their sins; and God gave to Iob double all his former goods, and even doubled them again. And all his brothers and his sisters heard what had happened to him; and they came to find him, and all those who knew him before that; and they ate and drank with him, and they rejoiced with him, and they were astonished at everything that God had made happen to him. And each one gave him a pair of oxen and gold and silver of the weight of four drachmas. And God blessed Iob, at the end more than at the beginning. And his beasts were: one hundred and four thousand sheep; and his camels one thousand; and his pairs of bulls one thousand; and his donkeys


[p. 687] {#po.2.p687}
ሠርቱ ፡ ምአት ። "ወተወልዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሰብዐቱ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ ወሠላስ ፡ አዋልድ ። "ወሰ መያ ፡ ለቀዳሚት ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወለካልእታ ፡ ቃስያን ፡ ወለሣልሲት ፣ አመልትያስ ፡ ቆራስ ። "ወኢተረክበ ፡ ክመ ፡ አዋልደ ፡ ኢየብ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ በታሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወወሀቦን ፡ ኢዮ ብ ፡ ርስቶን ፡ ምስለ ፡ አኃዊሆን ። ”ወሐይወ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ መቅሠፍቱ ምእ ት ፡ ወሰብዓ ፡ ክረምት ። ወኩሎ ፡ ዘሐይወ ፡ ክልኤ ፡ምአት ፡ ወአርባዓ ፡ ወሰማንቱ ፡፤ ዓመት ። ወርኢየ ፡ ኢየብ ፡ ደቂቆ ፡ ወደቂቀ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ራብዐ ፡ ትውልደ ። "ወሞተ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ልሂቆ ፡ ወፈጺሞ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ። ” ወጽሑፍ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትነሣእ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ም ስለ ፡ አለ ፡ ያነሥኦሙ- ፡ እግዚአ ፣ ብሔር ። "ወከመዝ ፣ ትርጓሜሁ ፡ እመጽሐፈ ፡ ሶር ያ ። ወብሔሩሰ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይነብር ፡ አውሷጢድ ፡ ደወለ ፡ ኢዶምያስ ፡ ዘዐረብ ። ወሰመይ ዎ፣፤ስሞ ፡፣ኢዮብ። "ወአውሰበ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ዓረባዊተ ። ወወለደት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወልደ ‹ዘ ስሙ ፡ ሔኖን ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡ ለዝሁራን ፡ ወልደ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ለኤሳው ፡ ወእ ሙሰ ፡ በሶራስ ፡ ወኃምስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምአብራሃም ። ”ወአሉ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ እለ ፡ ነግሠ ፡
one thousand. And there were born to him seven sons and three daughters; and he called the first Day, and the second Qasya, and the third Amaltyas Qoras. And one did not find any as beautiful as the daughters of Iob under the heaven; and Iob gave them their inheritance with their brothers. And Iob lived after his trial one hundred and seventy years; and all his life was two hundred and forty years; and Iob saw his sons, and the sons of his sons until the fourth generation. And Iob died old and having accomplished his days. And it is written that Iob will rise with those whom God will make rise. And such is the translation of the book of Syria. And his country, where Iob lived, was Aousetid, of the province of Edomyas of Arabia. And one named him by his name Iob; and he married an Arab woman; and she bore him a son, whose name was Henon; this one was the father of the proud, son of the son of Esau; and his mother was called Basoras; and he was the fifth from Abraham. And these are the kings who reigned in Edom, and he commanded


[p. 688] {#po.2.p688}
ለኤዶም ። ወውአቱሂ ፡ ኩነነ ፡ በሐውርተ ፡ ቀዳሚሁ ፡ ባለቅ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ቤዖር ፡ ወስ መ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ዲናባ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ባለቅ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ዘስሙ ፣፤ ኢዮባብ ። ወአምድኅሬ ሁ ፡ አሳም ፡ ዘነግሠ ፡ ለብሔረ ፡ ቴማን ። ወእምድኅሬሁ ፡ አዳድ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ባራድ፡ዘ ቀተሎሙ ፡ ለምድያም ፡ በሐቅለ ፡ ሞአብ ፡ ወስመ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ጌቴም ። "ወአለሂ ፣፡ መጽ ኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ አዕርክቲሁ ፡ ኤልፋዝ ፡ ዘእምደቂቀ ፡ ኤሳው ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ቴማን ። ወበልዳ ዶስ ፡ መስፍነ ፡ አውኬኔዎን ። ወሳፍር ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ አሜኔዎን ።
the countries; and the first was Balag, son of Beor, and the name of his city was Dinaba; and after Balaqg, was Iob, whose name was Iobab; and after him, Asam, who reigned over the country of Teman: and after him, Adad, son of Barad, who killed those of Madyam in the campaign of Moab, and the name of his city was Getem. And these three friends, who came to find him, were: Elephaz, who was of the sons of Esau, king of Teman; and Baldados, prince of Aouken; and Safar, king of Amen.
ዘኢዮብ።
[END OF THE BOOK] OF IOB.


[p. 689] {#po.2.p689}
